# services (F) {#sec-options-services-f}

## `services.factorio.admins` {#opt-services.factorio.admins}

List of player names which will be admin.

*Type:* list of string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:*

``` nix
[
  "username"
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix)

## `services.factorio.allowedPlayers` {#opt-services.factorio.allowedPlayers}

If non-empty, only these player names are allowed to connect. The game
will not be able to save any changes made in-game with the /whitelist
console command, though they will still take effect until the server
is restarted.

If empty, the whitelist defaults to open, but can be managed with the
in-game /whitelist console command (see: /help whitelist), which will
cause changes to be saved to the game's state directory (see also:
`stateDirName`).

*Type:* list of string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:*

``` nix
[
  "Rseding91"
  "Oxyd"
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix)

## `services.factorio.autosave-interval` {#opt-services.factorio.autosave-interval}

Autosave interval in minutes.

*Type:* null or signed integer

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `10`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix)

## `services.factorio.bind` {#opt-services.factorio.bind}

The address to which the service should bind.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"0.0.0.0"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix)

## `services.factorio.configFile` {#opt-services.factorio.configFile}

The server's configuration file.

The default file generated by this module contains lines essential to
the server's operation. Use its contents as a basis for any
customizations.

*Type:* absolute path

*Default:* `configFile`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix)

## `services.factorio.description` {#opt-services.factorio.description}

Description of the game that will appear in the listing.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `""`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix)

## `services.factorio.enable` {#opt-services.factorio.enable}

Whether to enable Factorio.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix)

## `services.factorio.extraArgs` {#opt-services.factorio.extraArgs}

Extra command line arguments.

*Type:* list of string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:*

``` nix
[
  "--rcon-bind=localhost:27015"
  "--rcon-password=..."
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix)

## `services.factorio.extraSettings` {#opt-services.factorio.extraSettings}

Extra game configuration that will go into server-settings.json

*Type:* attribute set

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  max_players = 64;
}
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix)

## `services.factorio.extraSettingsFile` {#opt-services.factorio.extraSettingsFile}

File, which is dynamically applied to server-settings.json before
startup.

This option should be used for credentials.

For example a settings file could contain:

``` json
{
  "game-password": "hunter1"
}
```

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix)

## `services.factorio.game-name` {#opt-services.factorio.game-name}

Name of the game as it will appear in the game listing.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `"Factorio Game"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix)

## `services.factorio.game-password` {#opt-services.factorio.game-password}

Game password.

This option is insecure. Use extraSettingsFile instead.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix)

## `services.factorio.lan` {#opt-services.factorio.lan}

Game will be broadcast on LAN.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix)

## `services.factorio.loadLatestSave` {#opt-services.factorio.loadLatestSave}

Load the latest savegame on startup. This overrides saveName, in that the latest
save will always be used even if a saved game of the given name exists. It still
controls the 'canonical' name of the savegame.

Set this to true to have the server automatically reload a recent autosave after
a crash or desync.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix)

## `services.factorio.mods` {#opt-services.factorio.mods}

Mods the server should install and activate.

The derivations in this list must "build" the mod by simply copying
the .zip, named correctly, into the output directory. Eventually,
there will be a way to pull in the most up-to-date list of
derivations via nixos-channel. Until then, this is for experts only.

*Type:* list of package

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix)

## `services.factorio.mods-dat` {#opt-services.factorio.mods-dat}

Mods settings can be changed by specifying a dat file, in the [mod
settings file
format](https://wiki.factorio.com/Mod_settings_file_format).

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix)

## `services.factorio.nonBlockingSaving` {#opt-services.factorio.nonBlockingSaving}

Highly experimental feature, enable only at your own risk of losing your saves.
On UNIX systems, server will fork itself to create an autosave.
Autosaving on connected Windows clients will be disabled regardless of autosave\_only\_on\_server option.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix)

## `services.factorio.openFirewall` {#opt-services.factorio.openFirewall}

Whether to automatically open the specified UDP port in the firewall.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix)

## `services.factorio.package` {#opt-services.factorio.package}

The factorio-headless package to use.

*Type:* package

*Default:* `pkgs.factorio-headless`

*Example:* `factorio-headless-experimental`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix)

## `services.factorio.password` {#opt-services.factorio.password}

Your factorio.com login credentials. Required for games with visibility public.

This option is insecure. Use extraSettingsFile instead.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix)

## `services.factorio.port` {#opt-services.factorio.port}

The port to which the service should bind.

*Type:* 16 bit unsigned integer; between 0 and 65535 (both inclusive)

*Default:* `34197`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix)

## `services.factorio.public` {#opt-services.factorio.public}

Game will be published on the official Factorio matching server.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix)

## `services.factorio.requireUserVerification` {#opt-services.factorio.requireUserVerification}

When set to true, the server will only allow clients that have a valid factorio.com account.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix)

## `services.factorio.saveName` {#opt-services.factorio.saveName}

The name of the savegame that will be used by the server.

When not present in /var/lib/${config.services.factorio.stateDirName}/saves,
a new map with default settings will be generated before starting the service.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"default"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix)

## `services.factorio.stateDirName` {#opt-services.factorio.stateDirName}

Name of the directory under /var/lib holding the server's data.

The configuration and map will be stored here.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"factorio"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix)

## `services.factorio.token` {#opt-services.factorio.token}

Authentication token. May be used instead of 'password' above.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix)

## `services.factorio.username` {#opt-services.factorio.username}

Your factorio.com login credentials. Required for games with visibility public.

This option is insecure. Use extraSettingsFile instead.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/games/factorio.nix)

## `services.fail2ban.banaction` {#opt-services.fail2ban.banaction}

Default banning action (e.g. iptables, iptables-new, iptables-multiport,
iptables-ipset-proto6-allports, shorewall, etc). It is used to
define action\_\* variables. Can be overridden globally or per
section within jail.local file

*Type:* string

*Default:* `if config.networking.nftables.enable then "nftables-multiport" else "iptables-multiport"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix)

## `services.fail2ban.banaction-allports` {#opt-services.fail2ban.banaction-allports}

Default banning action (e.g. iptables, iptables-new, iptables-multiport,
shorewall, etc) for "allports" jails. It is used to define action\_\* variables. Can be overridden
globally or per section within jail.local file

*Type:* string

*Default:* `if config.networking.nftables.enable then "nftables-allports" else "iptables-allports"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix)

## `services.fail2ban.bantime` {#opt-services.fail2ban.bantime}

Number of seconds that a host is banned.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"10m"`

*Example:* `"1h"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix)

## `services.fail2ban.bantime-increment.enable` {#opt-services.fail2ban.bantime-increment.enable}

"bantime.increment" allows to use database for searching of previously banned ip's to increase
a default ban time using special formula, default it is banTime \* 1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32 ...

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix)

## `services.fail2ban.bantime-increment.factor` {#opt-services.fail2ban.bantime-increment.factor}

"bantime.factor" is a coefficient to calculate exponent growing of the formula or common multiplier,
default value of factor is 1 and with default value of formula, the ban time grows by 1, 2, 4, 8, 16 ...

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"4"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix)

## `services.fail2ban.bantime-increment.formula` {#opt-services.fail2ban.bantime-increment.formula}

"bantime.formula" used by default to calculate next value of ban time, default value below,
the same ban time growing will be reached by multipliers 1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32 ...

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"ban.Time * math.exp(float(ban.Count+1)*banFactor)/math.exp(1*banFactor)"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix)

## `services.fail2ban.bantime-increment.maxtime` {#opt-services.fail2ban.bantime-increment.maxtime}

"bantime.maxtime" is the max number of seconds using the ban time can reach (don't grows further)

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"48h"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix)

## `services.fail2ban.bantime-increment.multipliers` {#opt-services.fail2ban.bantime-increment.multipliers}

"bantime.multipliers" used to calculate next value of ban time instead of formula, corresponding
previously ban count and given "bantime.factor" (for multipliers default is 1);
following example grows ban time by 1, 2, 4, 8, 16 ... and if last ban count greater as multipliers count,
always used last multiplier (64 in example), for factor '1' and original ban time 600 - 10.6 hours

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"1 2 4 8 16 32 64"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix)

## `services.fail2ban.bantime-increment.overalljails` {#opt-services.fail2ban.bantime-increment.overalljails}

"bantime.overalljails" (if true) specifies the search of IP in the database will be executed
cross over all jails, if false (default), only current jail of the ban IP will be searched.

*Type:* null or boolean

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix)

## `services.fail2ban.bantime-increment.rndtime` {#opt-services.fail2ban.bantime-increment.rndtime}

"bantime.rndtime" is the max number of seconds using for mixing with random time
to prevent "clever" botnets calculate exact time IP can be unbanned again

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"8m"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix)

## `services.fail2ban.daemonSettings` {#opt-services.fail2ban.daemonSettings}

The contents of Fail2ban's main configuration file.
It's generally not necessary to change it.

*Type:* attribute set of section of an INI file (attrs of INI atom (null, bool, int, float or string))

*Default:*

``` nix
{
  Definition = {
    logtarget = "SYSLOG";
    socket = "/run/fail2ban/fail2ban.sock";
    pidfile = "/run/fail2ban/fail2ban.pid";
    dbfile = "/var/lib/fail2ban/fail2ban.sqlite3";
  };
}
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix)

## `services.fail2ban.enable` {#opt-services.fail2ban.enable}

Whether to enable the fail2ban service.

See the documentation of [services (F)](#opt-services.fail2ban.jails)
for what jails are enabled by default.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix)

## `services.fail2ban.extraPackages` {#opt-services.fail2ban.extraPackages}

Extra packages to be made available to the fail2ban service. The example contains
the packages needed by the `iptables-ipset-proto6` action.

*Type:* list of package

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:* `[ pkgs.ipset ]`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix)

## `services.fail2ban.ignoreIP` {#opt-services.fail2ban.ignoreIP}

"ignoreIP" can be a list of IP addresses, CIDR masks or DNS hosts. Fail2ban will not ban a host which
matches an address in this list. Several addresses can be defined using space (and/or comma) separator.

*Type:* list of string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:*

``` nix
[
  "192.168.0.0/16"
  "2001:DB8::42"
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix)

## `services.fail2ban.jails` {#opt-services.fail2ban.jails}

The configuration of each Fail2ban “jail”.  A jail
consists of an action (such as blocking a port using
`iptables`) that is triggered when a
filter applied to a log file triggers more than a certain
number of times in a certain time period.  Actions are
defined in `/etc/fail2ban/action.d`,
while filters are defined in
`/etc/fail2ban/filter.d`.

NixOS comes with a default `sshd` jail;
for it to work well,
[services (O)](../../options/services-o/index.md#opt-services.openssh.settings.LogLevel) should be set to
`"VERBOSE"` or higher so that fail2ban
can observe failed login attempts.
This module sets it to `"VERBOSE"` if
not set otherwise, so enabling fail2ban can make SSH logs
more verbose.

*Type:* attribute set of (strings concatenated with "\\n" or (submodule))

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  apache-nohome-iptables = {
    settings = {
      # Block an IP address if it accesses a non-existent
      # home directory more than 5 times in 10 minutes,
      # since that indicates that it's scanning.
      filter = "apache-nohome";
      action = ''iptables-multiport[name=HTTP, port="http,https"]'';
      logpath = "/var/log/httpd/error_log*";
      backend = "auto";
      findtime = 600;
      bantime = 600;
      maxretry = 5;
    };
  };
  dovecot = {
    settings = {
      # block IPs which failed to log-in
      # aggressive mode add blocking for aborted connections
      filter = "dovecot[mode=aggressive]";
      maxretry = 3;
    };
  };
};
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix)

## `services.fail2ban.maxretry` {#opt-services.fail2ban.maxretry}

Number of failures before a host gets banned.

*Type:* unsigned integer, meaning \>=0

*Default:* `3`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix)

## `services.fail2ban.package` {#opt-services.fail2ban.package}

The fail2ban package to use.

*Type:* package

*Default:* `pkgs.fail2ban`

*Example:* `fail2ban_0_11`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix)

## `services.fail2ban.packageFirewall` {#opt-services.fail2ban.packageFirewall}

The firewall package used by fail2ban service. Defaults to the package for your firewall (iptables or nftables).

*Type:* package

*Default:* `config.networking.firewall.package`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/security/fail2ban.nix)

## `services.fakeroute.enable` {#opt-services.fakeroute.enable}

Whether to enable the fakeroute service.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fakeroute.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fakeroute.nix)

## `services.fakeroute.route` {#opt-services.fakeroute.route}

Fake route that will appear after the real
one to any host running a traceroute.

*Type:* list of string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:*

``` nix
[
  "216.102.187.130"
  "4.0.1.122"
  "198.116.142.34"
  "63.199.8.242"
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fakeroute.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fakeroute.nix)

## `services.falco.enable` {#opt-services.falco.enable}

Whether to enable Falco, a runtime security tool that detects unexpected behavior from kernel events.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/security/falco.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/security/falco.nix)

## `services.falco.package` {#opt-services.falco.package}

The falco package to use.

*Type:* package

*Default:* `pkgs.falco`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/security/falco.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/security/falco.nix)

## `services.falco.rules` {#opt-services.falco.rules}

Rule files, each given as the YAML list of rules, macros and lists that
Falco reads. The attribute name becomes the file name.

Files are loaded after the stock rules, in the lexical order of their
names. Overrides of a stock rule or macro (`override` or `append`) must
therefore come from here, not from the stock file itself. Prefix the
names with a number to control the order.

See the [rules reference](https://falco.org/docs/concepts/rules/) for
the format.

*Type:* attribute set of (YAML 1.1 value)

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  "50-local" = [
    {
      rule = "Write below /etc by a build";
      desc = "A build modified /etc";
      condition = "evt.type in (open, openat, openat2) and evt.is_open_write=true and fd.name startswith /etc/ and proc.name = nix-daemon";
      output = "Build wrote to /etc (file=%fd.name proc=%proc.name)";
      priority = "WARNING";
    }
  ];
}
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/security/falco.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/security/falco.nix)

## `services.falco.settings` {#opt-services.falco.settings}

Contents of `falco.yaml`. See the
[configuration reference](https://falco.org/docs/reference/daemon/config-options/)
for all keys.

Only what is set here is written, not the example file shipped with
Falco, so a key you leave out takes Falco's built-in default. The module
sets `rules_files`, `load_plugins` and `plugins` so that the stock rules
and the `container` plugin are loaded. Lists you add to these are
appended to them; use `lib.mkForce` to replace them.

Output goes to the journal by default. `syslog_output` and the
`webserver` are disabled, since the former would log every alert twice
and the latter listens on all interfaces upstream.

*Type:* YAML 1.1 value

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  json_output = true;
  priority = "warning";
  file_output = {
    enabled = true;
    filename = "/var/log/falco/events.json";
  };
}
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/security/falco.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/security/falco.nix)

## `services.fanout.bufferSize` {#opt-services.fanout.bufferSize}

Size of /dev/fanout buffer in bytes

## `services.fanout.fanoutDevices` {#opt-services.fanout.fanoutDevices}

## `services.fastnetmon-advanced.hostgroups` {#opt-services.fastnetmon-advanced.hostgroups}

Hostgroups to declaratively load into FastNetMon Advanced

*Type:* attribute set of (YAML 1.1 value)

*Default:* `{ }`

Extra configuration options to declaratively load into FastNetMon Advanced.

See the [FastNetMon Advanced Configuration options reference](https://fastnetmon.com/docs-fnm-advanced/fastnetmon-advanced-configuration-options/) for more details.

## `services.fcgiwrap.instances` {#opt-services.fcgiwrap.instances}

Configuration for fcgiwrap instances.

*Type:* attribute set of (submodule)

*Default:* `{ }`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-servers/fcgiwrap.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-servers/fcgiwrap.nix)

## `services.fcgiwrap.instances.<name>.process.group` {#opt-services.fcgiwrap.instances._name_.process.group}

Group as which this instance of fcgiwrap will be run.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-servers/fcgiwrap.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-servers/fcgiwrap.nix)

## `services.fcgiwrap.instances.<name>.process.prefork` {#opt-services.fcgiwrap.instances._name_.process.prefork}

Number of processes to prefork.

*Type:* positive integer, meaning \>0

*Default:* `1`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-servers/fcgiwrap.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-servers/fcgiwrap.nix)

## `services.fcgiwrap.instances.<name>.process.user` {#opt-services.fcgiwrap.instances._name_.process.user}

User as which this instance of fcgiwrap will be run.
Set to `null` (the default) to use a dynamically allocated user.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-servers/fcgiwrap.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-servers/fcgiwrap.nix)

## `services.fcgiwrap.instances.<name>.socket.address` {#opt-services.fcgiwrap.instances._name_.socket.address}

Socket address.
In case of a UNIX socket, this should be its filesystem path.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"/run/fcgiwrap-‹name›.sock"`

*Example:* `"1.2.3.4:5678"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-servers/fcgiwrap.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-servers/fcgiwrap.nix)

## `services.fcgiwrap.instances.<name>.socket.group` {#opt-services.fcgiwrap.instances._name_.socket.group}

Group to be set as owner of the UNIX socket.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-servers/fcgiwrap.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-servers/fcgiwrap.nix)

## `services.fcgiwrap.instances.<name>.socket.mode` {#opt-services.fcgiwrap.instances._name_.socket.mode}

Mode to be set on the UNIX socket.
Defaults to private to the socket's owner.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:*

``` nix
if config.socket.type == "unix" then "0600" else null
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-servers/fcgiwrap.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-servers/fcgiwrap.nix)

## `services.fcgiwrap.instances.<name>.socket.type` {#opt-services.fcgiwrap.instances._name_.socket.type}

Socket type: 'unix', 'tcp' or 'tcp6'.

*Type:* one of "unix", "tcp", "tcp6"

*Default:* `"unix"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-servers/fcgiwrap.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-servers/fcgiwrap.nix)

## `services.fcgiwrap.instances.<name>.socket.user` {#opt-services.fcgiwrap.instances._name_.socket.user}

User to be set as owner of the UNIX socket.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-servers/fcgiwrap.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-servers/fcgiwrap.nix)

## `services.fcron.allow` {#opt-services.fcron.allow}

Users allowed to use fcrontab and fcrondyn (one name per
line, `all` for everyone).

*Type:* list of string

*Default:*

``` nix
[
  "all"
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/scheduling/fcron.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/scheduling/fcron.nix)

## `services.fcron.deny` {#opt-services.fcron.deny}

Users forbidden from using fcron.

*Type:* list of string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/scheduling/fcron.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/scheduling/fcron.nix)

## `services.fcron.enable` {#opt-services.fcron.enable}

Whether to enable the `fcron` daemon.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/scheduling/fcron.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/scheduling/fcron.nix)

## `services.fcron.maxSerialJobs` {#opt-services.fcron.maxSerialJobs}

Maximum number of serial jobs which can run simultaneously.

*Type:* signed integer

*Default:* `1`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/scheduling/fcron.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/scheduling/fcron.nix)

## `services.fcron.queuelen` {#opt-services.fcron.queuelen}

Number of jobs the serial queue and the lavg queue can contain.

*Type:* null or signed integer

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/scheduling/fcron.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/scheduling/fcron.nix)

## `services.fcron.systab` {#opt-services.fcron.systab}

The "system" crontab contents.

*Type:* strings concatenated with "\\n"

*Default:* `""`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/scheduling/fcron.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/scheduling/fcron.nix)

## `services.fedimintd` {#opt-services.fedimintd}

Specification of one or more fedimintd instances.

*Type:* attribute set of (submodule)

*Default:* `{ }`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.api_iroh.bind` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.api_iroh.bind}

Address to bind on for Iroh endpoint for API connections

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"0.0.0.0"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.api_iroh.openFirewall` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.api_iroh.openFirewall}

Opens UDP port in firewall for fedimintd's API Iroh endpoint

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.api_iroh.port` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.api_iroh.port}

UDP Port to bind Iroh endpoint for API connections

*Type:* 16 bit unsigned integer; between 0 and 65535 (both inclusive)

*Default:* `8174`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.api_ws.bind` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.api_ws.bind}

Address to bind on for API connections relied by the reverse proxy/tls terminator.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"127.0.0.1"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.api_ws.openFirewall` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.api_ws.openFirewall}

Opens TCP port in firewall for fedimintd's Websocket API

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.api_ws.port` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.api_ws.port}

TCP Port to bind on for API connections relayed by the reverse proxy/tls terminator.

*Type:* 16 bit unsigned integer; between 0 and 65535 (both inclusive)

*Default:* `8174`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.api_ws.url` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.api_ws.url}

Public URL of the API address of the reverse proxy/tls terminator. Usually starting with `wss://`.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.bitcoin.network` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.bitcoin.network}

Bitcoin network to participate in.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"signet"`

*Example:* `"bitcoin"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.bitcoin.rpc.kind` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.bitcoin.rpc.kind}

Kind of a bitcoin node.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"bitcoind"`

*Example:* `"electrum"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.bitcoin.rpc.secretFile` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.bitcoin.rpc.secretFile}

If set the URL specified in `bitcoin.rpc.url` will get the content of this file added
as an URL password, so `http://user@example.com` will turn into `http://user:SOMESECRET@example.com`.

Example:

`/etc/nix-bitcoin-secrets/bitcoin-rpcpassword-public` (for nix-bitcoin default)

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.bitcoin.rpc.url` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.bitcoin.rpc.url}

Bitcoin node (bitcoind/electrum/esplora) address to connect to

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"http://127.0.0.1:38332"`

*Example:* `"signet"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.consensus.finalityDelay` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.consensus.finalityDelay}

Consensus peg-in finality delay.

*Type:* unsigned integer, meaning \>=0

*Default:* `10`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.dataDir` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.dataDir}

Path to the data dir fedimintd will use to store its data.
Note that due to using the DynamicUser feature of systemd, this value should not be changed
and is set to be read only.

*Type:* absolute path

*Default:* `"/var/lib/fedimintd-‹name›/"`

*Read only*

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.enable` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.enable}

Whether to enable fedimintd.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.environment` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.environment}

Extra Environment variables to pass to the fedimintd.

*Type:* attribute set of string

*Default:*

``` nix
{
  RUST_BACKTRACE = "1";
}
```

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  RUST_BACKTRACE = "1";
  RUST_LOG = "info,fm=debug";
}
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config}

Overrides to the nginx vhost section for api

*Type:* submodule

*Default:* `{ }`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.acmeFallbackHost` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.acmeFallbackHost}

Host which to proxy requests to if ACME challenge is not found. Useful
if you want multiple hosts to be able to verify the same domain name.

With this option, you could request certificates for the present domain
with an ACME client that is running on another host, which you would
specify here.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.acmeRoot` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.acmeRoot}

Directory for the ACME challenge, which is **public**. Don't put certs or keys in here.
Set to null to inherit from config.security.acme.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `"/var/lib/acme/acme-challenge"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.addSSL` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.addSSL}

Whether to enable HTTPS in addition to plain HTTP. This will set defaults for
`listen` to listen on all interfaces on the respective default
ports (80, 443).

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.basicAuth` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.basicAuth}

Basic Auth protection for a vhost.

WARNING: This is implemented to store the password in plain text in the
Nix store.

*Type:* attribute set of string

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  user = "password";
};
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.basicAuthFile` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.basicAuthFile}

Basic Auth password file for a vhost.
Can be created by running `nix-shell --packages apacheHttpd --run 'htpasswd -B -c FILENAME USERNAME'`.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.default` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.default}

Makes this vhost the default.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.enableACME` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.enableACME}

Whether to ask Let's Encrypt to sign a certificate for this vhost.
Alternately, you can use an existing certificate through `useACMEHost`.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.extraConfig` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.extraConfig}

These lines go to the end of the vhost verbatim.

*Type:* strings concatenated with "\\n"

*Default:* `""`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.forceSSL` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.forceSSL}

Whether to add a separate nginx server block that redirects (defaults
to 301, configurable with `redirectCode`) all plain HTTP traffic to
HTTPS. This will set defaults for `listen` to listen on all interfaces
on the respective default ports (80, 443), where the non-SSL listens
are used for the redirect vhosts.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.globalRedirect` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.globalRedirect}

If set, all requests for this host are redirected (defaults to 301,
configurable with `redirectCode`) to the given hostname.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"newserver.example.org"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.http2` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.http2}

Whether to enable the HTTP/2 protocol.
Note that (as of writing) due to nginx's implementation, to disable
HTTP/2 you have to disable it on all vhosts that use a given
IP address / port.
If there is one server block configured to enable http2, then it is
enabled for all server blocks on this IP.
See [https://stackoverflow.com/a/39466948/263061](https://stackoverflow.com/a/39466948/263061).

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.http3` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.http3}

Whether to enable the HTTP/3 protocol.
This requires activating the QUIC transport protocol
`services.nginx.virtualHosts.<name>.quic = true;`.
Note that HTTP/3 support is experimental and *not* yet recommended for production.
Read more at [https://quic.nginx.org/](https://quic.nginx.org/)
HTTP/3 availability must be manually advertised, preferably in each location block.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.http3_hq` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.http3_hq}

Whether to enable the HTTP/0.9 protocol negotiation used in QUIC interoperability tests.
This requires activating the QUIC transport protocol
`services.nginx.virtualHosts.<name>.quic = true;`.
Note that special application protocol support is experimental and *not* yet recommended for production.
Read more at [https://quic.nginx.org/](https://quic.nginx.org/)

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.kTLS` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.kTLS}

Whether to enable kTLS support.
Implementing TLS in the kernel (kTLS) improves performance by significantly
reducing the need for copying operations between user space and the kernel.
Required Nginx version 1.21.4 or later.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.listen` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.listen}

Listen addresses and ports for this virtual host.
IPv6 addresses must be enclosed in square brackets.
Note: this option overrides `addSSL`
and `onlySSL`.

If you only want to set the addresses manually and not
the ports, take a look at `listenAddresses`.

*Type:* list of (submodule)

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:*

``` nix
[
  {
    addr = "195.154.1.1";
    port = 443;
    ssl = true;
  }
  {
    addr = "192.154.1.1";
    port = 80;
  }
  {
    addr = "unix:/var/run/nginx.sock";
  }
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.listen.*.addr` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.listen._.addr}

Listen address.

*Type:* string

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.listen.*.extraParameters` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.listen._.extraParameters}

Extra parameters of this listen directive.

*Type:* list of string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:*

``` nix
[
  "backlog=1024"
  "deferred"
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.listen.*.port` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.listen._.port}

Port number to listen on.
If unset and the listen address is not a socket then nginx defaults to 80.

*Type:* null or 16 bit unsigned integer; between 0 and 65535 (both inclusive)

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.listen.*.proxyProtocol` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.listen._.proxyProtocol}

Enable PROXY protocol.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.listen.*.ssl` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.listen._.ssl}

Enable SSL.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.listenAddresses` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.listenAddresses}

Listen addresses for this virtual host.
Compared to `listen` this only sets the addresses
and the ports are chosen automatically.

Note: This option overrides `networking.enableIPv6`

*Type:* list of string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:*

``` nix
[
  "127.0.0.1"
  "[::1]"
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.locations` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.locations}

Declarative location config

*Type:* attribute set of (submodule)

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  "/" = {
    proxyPass = "http://localhost:3000";
  };
};
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.locations.<name>.alias` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.locations._name_.alias}

Alias directory for requests.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"/your/alias/directory"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.locations.<name>.basicAuth` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.locations._name_.basicAuth}

Basic Auth protection for a vhost.

WARNING: This is implemented to store the password in plain text in the
Nix store.

*Type:* attribute set of string

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  user = "password";
};
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.locations.<name>.basicAuthFile` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.locations._name_.basicAuthFile}

Basic Auth password file for a vhost.
Can be created by running `nix-shell --packages apacheHttpd --run 'htpasswd -B -c FILENAME USERNAME'`.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.locations.<name>.extraConfig` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.locations._name_.extraConfig}

These lines go to the end of the location verbatim.

*Type:* strings concatenated with "\\n"

*Default:* `""`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.locations.<name>.fastcgiParams` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.locations._name_.fastcgiParams}

FastCGI parameters to override.  Unlike in the Nginx
configuration file, overriding only some default parameters
won't unset the default values for other parameters.

*Type:* attribute set of (string or absolute path)

*Default:* `{ }`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.locations.<name>.index` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.locations._name_.index}

Adds index directive.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"index.php index.html"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.locations.<name>.priority` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.locations._name_.priority}

Order of this location block in relation to the others in the vhost.
The semantics are the same as with `lib.mkOrder`. Smaller values have
a greater priority.

*Type:* signed integer

*Default:* `1000`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.locations.<name>.proxyPass` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.locations._name_.proxyPass}

Adds proxy\_pass directive and sets recommended proxy headers if
recommendedProxySettings is enabled.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"http://www.example.org/"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.locations.<name>.proxyWebsockets` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.locations._name_.proxyWebsockets}

Whether to support proxying websocket connections with HTTP/1.1.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.locations.<name>.recommendedProxySettings` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.locations._name_.recommendedProxySettings}

Enable recommended proxy settings.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `config.services.nginx.recommendedProxySettings`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.locations.<name>.recommendedUwsgiSettings` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.locations._name_.recommendedUwsgiSettings}

Enable recommended uwsgi settings.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `config.services.nginx.recommendedUwsgiSettings`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.locations.<name>.return` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.locations._name_.return}

Adds a return directive, for e.g. redirections.

*Type:* null or string or signed integer

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"301 http://example.com$request_uri"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.locations.<name>.root` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.locations._name_.root}

Root directory for requests.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"/your/root/directory"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.locations.<name>.tryFiles` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.locations._name_.tryFiles}

Adds try\_files directive.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"$uri =404"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.locations.<name>.useGrpcErrorPages` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.locations._name_.useGrpcErrorPages}

Whether to configure error codes to be emitted as gRPC-compatible errors.

Should be set when proxying gRPC, and returning responses from nginx (like when adding authentication).

This defines a few `@grpc-*` locations inside the containing vhost.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.locations.<name>.uwsgiPass` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.locations._name_.uwsgiPass}

Adds uwsgi\_pass directive and sets recommended proxy headers if
recommendedUwsgiSettings is enabled.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"unix:/run/example/example.sock"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.onlySSL` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.onlySSL}

Whether to enable HTTPS and reject plain HTTP connections. This will set
defaults for `listen` to listen on all interfaces on port 443.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.quic` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.quic}

Whether to enable the QUIC transport protocol.
Note that QUIC support is experimental and
*not* yet recommended for production.
Read more at [https://quic.nginx.org/](https://quic.nginx.org/)

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.redirectCode` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.redirectCode}

HTTP status used by `globalRedirect` and `forceSSL`. Possible usecases
include temporary (302, 307) redirects, keeping the request method and
body (307, 308), or explicitly resetting the method to GET (303).
See [https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTTP/Redirections](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTTP/Redirections).

*Type:* integer between 300 and 399 (both inclusive)

*Default:* `301`

*Example:* `308`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.rejectSSL` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.rejectSSL}

Whether to listen for and reject all HTTPS connections to this vhost. Useful in
[default](../../options/services-n/index.md#opt-services.nginx.virtualHosts._name_.default)
server blocks to avoid serving the certificate for another vhost. Uses the
`ssl_reject_handshake` directive available in nginx versions
1.19.4 and above.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.reuseport` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.reuseport}

Create an individual listening socket .
It is required to specify only once on one of the hosts.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.root` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.root}

The path of the web root directory.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"/data/webserver/docs"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.serverAliases` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.serverAliases}

Additional names of virtual hosts served by this virtual host configuration.

*Type:* list of string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:*

``` nix
[
  "www.example.org"
  "example.org"
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.serverName` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.serverName}

Name of this virtual host. Defaults to attribute name in virtualHosts.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"example.org"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.sslCertificate` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.sslCertificate}

Path to server SSL certificate.

*Type:* absolute path

*Example:* `"/var/host.cert"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.sslCertificateKey` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.sslCertificateKey}

Path to server SSL certificate key.

*Type:* absolute path

*Example:* `"/var/host.key"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.sslTrustedCertificate` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.sslTrustedCertificate}

Path to root SSL certificate for stapling and client certificates.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"${pkgs.cacert}/etc/ssl/certs/ca-bundle.crt"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.config.useACMEHost` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.config.useACMEHost}

A host of an existing Let's Encrypt certificate to use.
This is useful if you have many subdomains and want to avoid hitting the
[rate limit](https://letsencrypt.org/docs/rate-limits).
Alternately, you can generate a certificate through `enableACME`.
*Note that this option does not create any certificates, nor it does add subdomains to existing ones – you will need to create them manually using [security](../../options/security/index.md#opt-security.acme.certs).*

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.enable` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.enable}

Whether to configure nginx for fedimintd

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.fqdn` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.fqdn}

Public domain of the API address of the reverse proxy/tls terminator.

*Type:* string

*Example:* `"api.myfedimint.com"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.path_ui` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.path_ui}

Path to host the built-in UI on and forward to the daemon's api port

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"/"`

*Example:* `"/"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.nginx.path_ws` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.nginx.path_ws}

Path to host the API on and forward to the daemon's api port

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"/ws/"`

*Example:* `"/"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.p2p.bind` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.p2p.bind}

Address to bind on for p2p connections from peers (both TCP and UDP)

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"0.0.0.0"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.p2p.openFirewall` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.p2p.openFirewall}

Opens port in firewall for fedimintd's p2p port (both TCP and UDP)

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.p2p.port` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.p2p.port}

Port to bind on for p2p connections from peers (both TCP and UDP)

*Type:* 16 bit unsigned integer; between 0 and 65535 (both inclusive)

*Default:* `8173`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.p2p.url` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.p2p.url}

Public address for p2p connections from peers (if TCP is used)

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"fedimint://p2p.myfedimint.com:8173"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.package` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.package}

The fedimint package to use.

*Type:* package

*Default:* `pkgs.fedimint`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.ui.bind` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.ui.bind}

Address to bind on for UI connections

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"127.0.0.1"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.ui.openFirewall` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.ui.openFirewall}

Opens TCP port in firewall for built-in UI

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fedimintd.<name>.ui.port` {#opt-services.fedimintd._name_.ui.port}

TCP Port to bind on for UI connections

*Type:* 16 bit unsigned integer; between 0 and 65535 (both inclusive)

*Default:* `8175`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fedimintd.nix)

## `services.fediwall.enable` {#opt-services.fediwall.enable}

Whether to enable fediwall, a social media wall for the fediverse.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.hostName` {#opt-services.fediwall.hostName}

The hostname to serve fediwall on.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `config.networking.fqdnOrHostName`

*Example:* `"fediwall.example.org"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx}

Allows customizing the nginx virtualHost settings

*Type:* submodule

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  serverAliases = [
    "fedi.${config.networking.domain}"
  ];
  # Enable TLS and use let's encrypt for ACME
  forceSSL = true;
  enableACME = true;
}
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.acmeFallbackHost` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.acmeFallbackHost}

Host which to proxy requests to if ACME challenge is not found. Useful
if you want multiple hosts to be able to verify the same domain name.

With this option, you could request certificates for the present domain
with an ACME client that is running on another host, which you would
specify here.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.acmeRoot` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.acmeRoot}

Directory for the ACME challenge, which is **public**. Don't put certs or keys in here.
Set to null to inherit from config.security.acme.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `"/var/lib/acme/acme-challenge"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.addSSL` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.addSSL}

Whether to enable HTTPS in addition to plain HTTP. This will set defaults for
`listen` to listen on all interfaces on the respective default
ports (80, 443).

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.basicAuth` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.basicAuth}

Basic Auth protection for a vhost.

WARNING: This is implemented to store the password in plain text in the
Nix store.

*Type:* attribute set of string

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  user = "password";
};
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.basicAuthFile` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.basicAuthFile}

Basic Auth password file for a vhost.
Can be created by running `nix-shell --packages apacheHttpd --run 'htpasswd -B -c FILENAME USERNAME'`.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.default` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.default}

Makes this vhost the default.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.enableACME` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.enableACME}

Whether to ask Let's Encrypt to sign a certificate for this vhost.
Alternately, you can use an existing certificate through `useACMEHost`.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.extraConfig` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.extraConfig}

These lines go to the end of the vhost verbatim.

*Type:* strings concatenated with "\\n"

*Default:* `""`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.forceSSL` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.forceSSL}

Whether to add a separate nginx server block that redirects (defaults
to 301, configurable with `redirectCode`) all plain HTTP traffic to
HTTPS. This will set defaults for `listen` to listen on all interfaces
on the respective default ports (80, 443), where the non-SSL listens
are used for the redirect vhosts.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.globalRedirect` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.globalRedirect}

If set, all requests for this host are redirected (defaults to 301,
configurable with `redirectCode`) to the given hostname.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"newserver.example.org"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.http2` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.http2}

Whether to enable the HTTP/2 protocol.
Note that (as of writing) due to nginx's implementation, to disable
HTTP/2 you have to disable it on all vhosts that use a given
IP address / port.
If there is one server block configured to enable http2, then it is
enabled for all server blocks on this IP.
See [https://stackoverflow.com/a/39466948/263061](https://stackoverflow.com/a/39466948/263061).

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.http3` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.http3}

Whether to enable the HTTP/3 protocol.
This requires activating the QUIC transport protocol
`services.nginx.virtualHosts.<name>.quic = true;`.
Note that HTTP/3 support is experimental and *not* yet recommended for production.
Read more at [https://quic.nginx.org/](https://quic.nginx.org/)
HTTP/3 availability must be manually advertised, preferably in each location block.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.http3_hq` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.http3_hq}

Whether to enable the HTTP/0.9 protocol negotiation used in QUIC interoperability tests.
This requires activating the QUIC transport protocol
`services.nginx.virtualHosts.<name>.quic = true;`.
Note that special application protocol support is experimental and *not* yet recommended for production.
Read more at [https://quic.nginx.org/](https://quic.nginx.org/)

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.kTLS` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.kTLS}

Whether to enable kTLS support.
Implementing TLS in the kernel (kTLS) improves performance by significantly
reducing the need for copying operations between user space and the kernel.
Required Nginx version 1.21.4 or later.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.listen` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.listen}

Listen addresses and ports for this virtual host.
IPv6 addresses must be enclosed in square brackets.
Note: this option overrides `addSSL`
and `onlySSL`.

If you only want to set the addresses manually and not
the ports, take a look at `listenAddresses`.

*Type:* list of (submodule)

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:*

``` nix
[
  {
    addr = "195.154.1.1";
    port = 443;
    ssl = true;
  }
  {
    addr = "192.154.1.1";
    port = 80;
  }
  {
    addr = "unix:/var/run/nginx.sock";
  }
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.listen.*.addr` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.listen._.addr}

Listen address.

*Type:* string

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.listen.*.extraParameters` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.listen._.extraParameters}

Extra parameters of this listen directive.

*Type:* list of string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:*

``` nix
[
  "backlog=1024"
  "deferred"
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.listen.*.port` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.listen._.port}

Port number to listen on.
If unset and the listen address is not a socket then nginx defaults to 80.

*Type:* null or 16 bit unsigned integer; between 0 and 65535 (both inclusive)

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.listen.*.proxyProtocol` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.listen._.proxyProtocol}

Enable PROXY protocol.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.listen.*.ssl` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.listen._.ssl}

Enable SSL.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.listenAddresses` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.listenAddresses}

Listen addresses for this virtual host.
Compared to `listen` this only sets the addresses
and the ports are chosen automatically.

Note: This option overrides `networking.enableIPv6`

*Type:* list of string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:*

``` nix
[
  "127.0.0.1"
  "[::1]"
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.locations` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.locations}

Declarative location config

*Type:* attribute set of (submodule)

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  "/" = {
    proxyPass = "http://localhost:3000";
  };
};
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.locations.<name>.alias` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.locations._name_.alias}

Alias directory for requests.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"/your/alias/directory"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.locations.<name>.basicAuth` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.locations._name_.basicAuth}

Basic Auth protection for a vhost.

WARNING: This is implemented to store the password in plain text in the
Nix store.

*Type:* attribute set of string

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  user = "password";
};
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.locations.<name>.basicAuthFile` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.locations._name_.basicAuthFile}

Basic Auth password file for a vhost.
Can be created by running `nix-shell --packages apacheHttpd --run 'htpasswd -B -c FILENAME USERNAME'`.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.locations.<name>.extraConfig` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.locations._name_.extraConfig}

These lines go to the end of the location verbatim.

*Type:* strings concatenated with "\\n"

*Default:* `""`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.locations.<name>.fastcgiParams` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.locations._name_.fastcgiParams}

FastCGI parameters to override.  Unlike in the Nginx
configuration file, overriding only some default parameters
won't unset the default values for other parameters.

*Type:* attribute set of (string or absolute path)

*Default:* `{ }`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.locations.<name>.index` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.locations._name_.index}

Adds index directive.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`
