*Example:* `"index.php index.html"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.locations.<name>.priority` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.locations._name_.priority}

Order of this location block in relation to the others in the vhost.
The semantics are the same as with `lib.mkOrder`. Smaller values have
a greater priority.

*Type:* signed integer

*Default:* `1000`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.locations.<name>.proxyPass` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.locations._name_.proxyPass}

Adds proxy\_pass directive and sets recommended proxy headers if
recommendedProxySettings is enabled.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"http://www.example.org/"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.locations.<name>.proxyWebsockets` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.locations._name_.proxyWebsockets}

Whether to support proxying websocket connections with HTTP/1.1.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.locations.<name>.recommendedProxySettings` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.locations._name_.recommendedProxySettings}

Enable recommended proxy settings.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `config.services.nginx.recommendedProxySettings`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.locations.<name>.recommendedUwsgiSettings` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.locations._name_.recommendedUwsgiSettings}

Enable recommended uwsgi settings.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `config.services.nginx.recommendedUwsgiSettings`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.locations.<name>.return` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.locations._name_.return}

Adds a return directive, for e.g. redirections.

*Type:* null or string or signed integer

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"301 http://example.com$request_uri"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.locations.<name>.root` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.locations._name_.root}

Root directory for requests.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"/your/root/directory"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.locations.<name>.tryFiles` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.locations._name_.tryFiles}

Adds try\_files directive.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"$uri =404"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.locations.<name>.useGrpcErrorPages` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.locations._name_.useGrpcErrorPages}

Whether to configure error codes to be emitted as gRPC-compatible errors.

Should be set when proxying gRPC, and returning responses from nginx (like when adding authentication).

This defines a few `@grpc-*` locations inside the containing vhost.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.locations.<name>.uwsgiPass` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.locations._name_.uwsgiPass}

Adds uwsgi\_pass directive and sets recommended proxy headers if
recommendedUwsgiSettings is enabled.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"unix:/run/example/example.sock"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.onlySSL` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.onlySSL}

Whether to enable HTTPS and reject plain HTTP connections. This will set
defaults for `listen` to listen on all interfaces on port 443.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.quic` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.quic}

Whether to enable the QUIC transport protocol.
Note that QUIC support is experimental and
*not* yet recommended for production.
Read more at [https://quic.nginx.org/](https://quic.nginx.org/)

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.redirectCode` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.redirectCode}

HTTP status used by `globalRedirect` and `forceSSL`. Possible usecases
include temporary (302, 307) redirects, keeping the request method and
body (307, 308), or explicitly resetting the method to GET (303).
See [https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTTP/Redirections](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTTP/Redirections).

*Type:* integer between 300 and 399 (both inclusive)

*Default:* `301`

*Example:* `308`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.rejectSSL` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.rejectSSL}

Whether to listen for and reject all HTTPS connections to this vhost. Useful in
[default](../../options/services-n/index.md#opt-services.nginx.virtualHosts._name_.default)
server blocks to avoid serving the certificate for another vhost. Uses the
`ssl_reject_handshake` directive available in nginx versions
1.19.4 and above.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.reuseport` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.reuseport}

Create an individual listening socket .
It is required to specify only once on one of the hosts.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.root` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.root}

The path of the web root directory.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"/data/webserver/docs"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.serverAliases` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.serverAliases}

Additional names of virtual hosts served by this virtual host configuration.

*Type:* list of string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:*

``` nix
[
  "www.example.org"
  "example.org"
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.serverName` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.serverName}

Name of this virtual host. Defaults to attribute name in virtualHosts.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"example.org"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.sslCertificate` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.sslCertificate}

Path to server SSL certificate.

*Type:* absolute path

*Example:* `"/var/host.cert"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.sslCertificateKey` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.sslCertificateKey}

Path to server SSL certificate key.

*Type:* absolute path

*Example:* `"/var/host.key"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.sslTrustedCertificate` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.sslTrustedCertificate}

Path to root SSL certificate for stapling and client certificates.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"${pkgs.cacert}/etc/ssl/certs/ca-bundle.crt"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.nginx.useACMEHost` {#opt-services.fediwall.nginx.useACMEHost}

A host of an existing Let's Encrypt certificate to use.
This is useful if you have many subdomains and want to avoid hitting the
[rate limit](https://letsencrypt.org/docs/rate-limits).
Alternately, you can generate a certificate through `enableACME`.
*Note that this option does not create any certificates, nor it does add subdomains to existing ones – you will need to create them manually using [security](../../options/security/index.md#opt-security.acme.certs).*

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.package` {#opt-services.fediwall.package}

The fediwall package to use.

*Type:* package

*Default:* `pkgs.fediwall`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.settings` {#opt-services.fediwall.settings}

Fediwall configuration. See
https://github.com/defnull/fediwall/blob/main/public/wall-config.json.example
for information on supported values.

*Type:* open submodule of (JSON value)

*Default:* `{ }`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.settings.hideBoosts` {#opt-services.fediwall.settings.hideBoosts}

Hide boosts

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.settings.hideBots` {#opt-services.fediwall.settings.hideBots}

Hide posts from bot accounts

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.settings.hideReplies` {#opt-services.fediwall.settings.hideReplies}

Hide replies

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.settings.hideSensitive` {#opt-services.fediwall.settings.hideSensitive}

Hide sensitive (potentially NSFW) posts

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.settings.loadFederated` {#opt-services.fediwall.settings.loadFederated}

Load federated posts

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.settings.loadPublic` {#opt-services.fediwall.settings.loadPublic}

Load public posts

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.settings.loadTrends` {#opt-services.fediwall.settings.loadTrends}

Load trending posts

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.settings.playVideos` {#opt-services.fediwall.settings.playVideos}

Autoplay videos in posts

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.settings.servers` {#opt-services.fediwall.settings.servers}

Servers to load posts from

*Type:* list of string

*Default:*

``` nix
[
  "mastodon.social"
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.settings.showMedia` {#opt-services.fediwall.settings.showMedia}

Show media in posts

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.fediwall.settings.tags` {#opt-services.fediwall.settings.tags}

Tags to follow

*Type:* list of string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:* `[ "cats" "dogs"]`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fediwall.nix)

## `services.feishin.caddy.enable` {#opt-services.feishin.caddy.enable}

Whether to enable a virtualhost to serve Feishin through Caddy.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.caddy.virtualHost` {#opt-services.feishin.caddy.virtualHost}

Extra configuration for the Caddy virtualhost for Feishin.

*Type:* submodule

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  serverAliases = [ "feishin.${config.networking.domain}" ];
}
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.caddy.virtualHost.extraConfig` {#opt-services.feishin.caddy.virtualHost.extraConfig}

Additional lines of configuration appended to this virtual host in the
automatically generated `Caddyfile`.

*Type:* strings concatenated with "\\n"

*Default:* `""`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.caddy.virtualHost.hostName` {#opt-services.feishin.caddy.virtualHost.hostName}

Canonical hostname for the server.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"‹name›"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.caddy.virtualHost.listenAddresses` {#opt-services.feishin.caddy.virtualHost.listenAddresses}

A list of host interfaces to bind to for this virtual host.

*Type:* list of string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:*

``` nix
[
  "127.0.0.1"
  "::1"
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.caddy.virtualHost.logFormat` {#opt-services.feishin.caddy.virtualHost.logFormat}

Configuration for HTTP request logging (also known as access logs). See
[https://caddyserver.com/docs/caddyfile/directives/log#log](https://caddyserver.com/docs/caddyfile/directives/log#log)
for details.

*Type:* null or strings concatenated with "\\n"

*Default:*

``` nix
''
  output file ''${config.services.caddy.logDir}/access-''${hostName}.log
''
```

*Example:*

``` nix
mkForce ''
  output discard
'';
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.caddy.virtualHost.serverAliases` {#opt-services.feishin.caddy.virtualHost.serverAliases}

Additional names of virtual hosts served by this virtual host configuration.

*Type:* list of string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:*

``` nix
[
  "www.example.org"
  "example.org"
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.caddy.virtualHost.useACMEHost` {#opt-services.feishin.caddy.virtualHost.useACMEHost}

A host of an existing Let's Encrypt certificate to use.
This is mostly useful if you use DNS challenges but Caddy does not
currently support your provider.

*Note that this option does not create any certificates, nor
does it add subdomains to existing ones – you will need to create them
manually using [security](../../options/security/index.md#opt-security.acme.certs).*

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.domain` {#opt-services.feishin.domain}

Domain to use for the virtualhost.

*Type:* string

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.enable` {#opt-services.feishin.enable}

Whether to enable the web version of Feishin.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.enable` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.enable}

Whether to enable a virtualhost to serve Feishin through nginx.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost}

Extra configuration for the nginx virtualhost for Feishin.

*Type:* submodule

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  serverAliases = [ "feishin.${config.networking.domain}" ];
}
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.acmeFallbackHost` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.acmeFallbackHost}

Host which to proxy requests to if ACME challenge is not found. Useful
if you want multiple hosts to be able to verify the same domain name.

With this option, you could request certificates for the present domain
with an ACME client that is running on another host, which you would
specify here.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.acmeRoot` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.acmeRoot}

Directory for the ACME challenge, which is **public**. Don't put certs or keys in here.
Set to null to inherit from config.security.acme.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `"/var/lib/acme/acme-challenge"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.addSSL` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.addSSL}

Whether to enable HTTPS in addition to plain HTTP. This will set defaults for
`listen` to listen on all interfaces on the respective default
ports (80, 443).

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.basicAuth` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.basicAuth}

Basic Auth protection for a vhost.

WARNING: This is implemented to store the password in plain text in the
Nix store.

*Type:* attribute set of string

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  user = "password";
};
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.basicAuthFile` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.basicAuthFile}

Basic Auth password file for a vhost.
Can be created by running `nix-shell --packages apacheHttpd --run 'htpasswd -B -c FILENAME USERNAME'`.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.default` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.default}

Makes this vhost the default.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.enableACME` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.enableACME}

Whether to ask Let's Encrypt to sign a certificate for this vhost.
Alternately, you can use an existing certificate through `useACMEHost`.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.extraConfig` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.extraConfig}

These lines go to the end of the vhost verbatim.

*Type:* strings concatenated with "\\n"

*Default:* `""`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.forceSSL` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.forceSSL}

Whether to add a separate nginx server block that redirects (defaults
to 301, configurable with `redirectCode`) all plain HTTP traffic to
HTTPS. This will set defaults for `listen` to listen on all interfaces
on the respective default ports (80, 443), where the non-SSL listens
are used for the redirect vhosts.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.globalRedirect` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.globalRedirect}

If set, all requests for this host are redirected (defaults to 301,
configurable with `redirectCode`) to the given hostname.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"newserver.example.org"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.http2` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.http2}

Whether to enable the HTTP/2 protocol.
Note that (as of writing) due to nginx's implementation, to disable
HTTP/2 you have to disable it on all vhosts that use a given
IP address / port.
If there is one server block configured to enable http2, then it is
enabled for all server blocks on this IP.
See [https://stackoverflow.com/a/39466948/263061](https://stackoverflow.com/a/39466948/263061).

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.http3` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.http3}

Whether to enable the HTTP/3 protocol.
This requires activating the QUIC transport protocol
`services.nginx.virtualHosts.<name>.quic = true;`.
Note that HTTP/3 support is experimental and *not* yet recommended for production.
Read more at [https://quic.nginx.org/](https://quic.nginx.org/)
HTTP/3 availability must be manually advertised, preferably in each location block.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.http3_hq` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.http3_hq}

Whether to enable the HTTP/0.9 protocol negotiation used in QUIC interoperability tests.
This requires activating the QUIC transport protocol
`services.nginx.virtualHosts.<name>.quic = true;`.
Note that special application protocol support is experimental and *not* yet recommended for production.
Read more at [https://quic.nginx.org/](https://quic.nginx.org/)

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.kTLS` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.kTLS}

Whether to enable kTLS support.
Implementing TLS in the kernel (kTLS) improves performance by significantly
reducing the need for copying operations between user space and the kernel.
Required Nginx version 1.21.4 or later.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.listen` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.listen}

Listen addresses and ports for this virtual host.
IPv6 addresses must be enclosed in square brackets.
Note: this option overrides `addSSL`
and `onlySSL`.

If you only want to set the addresses manually and not
the ports, take a look at `listenAddresses`.

*Type:* list of (submodule)

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:*

``` nix
[
  {
    addr = "195.154.1.1";
    port = 443;
    ssl = true;
  }
  {
    addr = "192.154.1.1";
    port = 80;
  }
  {
    addr = "unix:/var/run/nginx.sock";
  }
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.listen.*.addr` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.listen._.addr}

Listen address.

*Type:* string

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.listen.*.extraParameters` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.listen._.extraParameters}

Extra parameters of this listen directive.

*Type:* list of string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:*

``` nix
[
  "backlog=1024"
  "deferred"
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.listen.*.port` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.listen._.port}

Port number to listen on.
If unset and the listen address is not a socket then nginx defaults to 80.

*Type:* null or 16 bit unsigned integer; between 0 and 65535 (both inclusive)

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.listen.*.proxyProtocol` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.listen._.proxyProtocol}

Enable PROXY protocol.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.listen.*.ssl` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.listen._.ssl}

Enable SSL.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.listenAddresses` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.listenAddresses}

Listen addresses for this virtual host.
Compared to `listen` this only sets the addresses
and the ports are chosen automatically.

Note: This option overrides `networking.enableIPv6`

*Type:* list of string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:*

``` nix
[
  "127.0.0.1"
  "[::1]"
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations}

Declarative location config

*Type:* attribute set of (submodule)

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  "/" = {
    proxyPass = "http://localhost:3000";
  };
};
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations.<name>.alias` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations._name_.alias}

Alias directory for requests.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"/your/alias/directory"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations.<name>.basicAuth` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations._name_.basicAuth}

Basic Auth protection for a vhost.

WARNING: This is implemented to store the password in plain text in the
Nix store.

*Type:* attribute set of string

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  user = "password";
};
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations.<name>.basicAuthFile` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations._name_.basicAuthFile}

Basic Auth password file for a vhost.
Can be created by running `nix-shell --packages apacheHttpd --run 'htpasswd -B -c FILENAME USERNAME'`.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations.<name>.extraConfig` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations._name_.extraConfig}

These lines go to the end of the location verbatim.

*Type:* strings concatenated with "\\n"

*Default:* `""`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations.<name>.fastcgiParams` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations._name_.fastcgiParams}

FastCGI parameters to override.  Unlike in the Nginx
configuration file, overriding only some default parameters
won't unset the default values for other parameters.

*Type:* attribute set of (string or absolute path)

*Default:* `{ }`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations.<name>.index` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations._name_.index}

Adds index directive.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"index.php index.html"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations.<name>.priority` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations._name_.priority}

Order of this location block in relation to the others in the vhost.
The semantics are the same as with `lib.mkOrder`. Smaller values have
a greater priority.

*Type:* signed integer

*Default:* `1000`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations.<name>.proxyPass` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations._name_.proxyPass}

Adds proxy\_pass directive and sets recommended proxy headers if
recommendedProxySettings is enabled.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"http://www.example.org/"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations.<name>.proxyWebsockets` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations._name_.proxyWebsockets}

Whether to support proxying websocket connections with HTTP/1.1.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations.<name>.recommendedProxySettings` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations._name_.recommendedProxySettings}

Enable recommended proxy settings.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `config.services.nginx.recommendedProxySettings`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations.<name>.recommendedUwsgiSettings` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations._name_.recommendedUwsgiSettings}

Enable recommended uwsgi settings.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `config.services.nginx.recommendedUwsgiSettings`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations.<name>.return` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations._name_.return}

Adds a return directive, for e.g. redirections.

*Type:* null or string or signed integer

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"301 http://example.com$request_uri"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations.<name>.root` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations._name_.root}

Root directory for requests.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"/your/root/directory"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations.<name>.tryFiles` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations._name_.tryFiles}

Adds try\_files directive.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"$uri =404"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations.<name>.useGrpcErrorPages` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations._name_.useGrpcErrorPages}

Whether to configure error codes to be emitted as gRPC-compatible errors.

Should be set when proxying gRPC, and returning responses from nginx (like when adding authentication).

This defines a few `@grpc-*` locations inside the containing vhost.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations.<name>.uwsgiPass` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.locations._name_.uwsgiPass}

Adds uwsgi\_pass directive and sets recommended proxy headers if
recommendedUwsgiSettings is enabled.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"unix:/run/example/example.sock"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.onlySSL` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.onlySSL}

Whether to enable HTTPS and reject plain HTTP connections. This will set
defaults for `listen` to listen on all interfaces on port 443.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.quic` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.quic}

Whether to enable the QUIC transport protocol.
Note that QUIC support is experimental and
*not* yet recommended for production.
Read more at [https://quic.nginx.org/](https://quic.nginx.org/)

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.redirectCode` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.redirectCode}

HTTP status used by `globalRedirect` and `forceSSL`. Possible usecases
include temporary (302, 307) redirects, keeping the request method and
body (307, 308), or explicitly resetting the method to GET (303).
See [https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTTP/Redirections](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTTP/Redirections).

*Type:* integer between 300 and 399 (both inclusive)

*Default:* `301`

*Example:* `308`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.rejectSSL` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.rejectSSL}

Whether to listen for and reject all HTTPS connections to this vhost. Useful in
[default](../../options/services-n/index.md#opt-services.nginx.virtualHosts._name_.default)
server blocks to avoid serving the certificate for another vhost. Uses the
`ssl_reject_handshake` directive available in nginx versions
1.19.4 and above.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.reuseport` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.reuseport}

Create an individual listening socket .
It is required to specify only once on one of the hosts.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.root` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.root}

The path of the web root directory.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"/data/webserver/docs"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.serverAliases` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.serverAliases}

Additional names of virtual hosts served by this virtual host configuration.

*Type:* list of string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:*

``` nix
[
  "www.example.org"
  "example.org"
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.serverName` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.serverName}

Name of this virtual host. Defaults to attribute name in virtualHosts.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"example.org"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.sslCertificate` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.sslCertificate}

Path to server SSL certificate.

*Type:* absolute path

*Example:* `"/var/host.cert"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.sslCertificateKey` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.sslCertificateKey}

Path to server SSL certificate key.

*Type:* absolute path

*Example:* `"/var/host.key"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.sslTrustedCertificate` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.sslTrustedCertificate}

Path to root SSL certificate for stapling and client certificates.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"${pkgs.cacert}/etc/ssl/certs/ca-bundle.crt"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.useACMEHost` {#opt-services.feishin.nginx.virtualHost.useACMEHost}

A host of an existing Let's Encrypt certificate to use.
This is useful if you have many subdomains and want to avoid hitting the
[rate limit](https://letsencrypt.org/docs/rate-limits).
Alternately, you can generate a certificate through `enableACME`.
*Note that this option does not create any certificates, nor it does add subdomains to existing ones – you will need to create them manually using [security](../../options/security/index.md#opt-security.acme.certs).*

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.package` {#opt-services.feishin.package}

The feishin-web package to use.

*Type:* package

*Default:* `pkgs.feishin-web`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.pathbase` {#opt-services.feishin.pathbase}

URL path base to use for the virtualhost.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `""`

*Example:* `"/music"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.feishin.settings` {#opt-services.feishin.settings}

Configuration for the web version of Feishin, specified as key-value pairs.

Refer to [https://github.com/jeffvli/feishin#configuration](https://github.com/jeffvli/feishin#configuration), and
[https://github.com/jeffvli/feishin/blob/development/settings.js.template](https://github.com/jeffvli/feishin/blob/development/settings.js.template)
for the template.

*Type:* attribute set of string

*Default:*

``` nix
{
  ANALYTICS_DISABLED = "true";
}
```

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  ANALYTICS_DISABLED = "true";
  SERVER_LOCK = "true";
  SERVER_NAME = "My Server";
}
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/feishin.nix)

## `services.felix.bundles` {#opt-services.felix.bundles}

List of bundles that should be activated on startup

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/misc/felix.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/misc/felix.nix)

Whether to enable the Apache Felix OSGi service.

## `services.felix.group` {#opt-services.felix.group}

## `services.felix.user` {#opt-services.felix.user}

User account under which Apache Felix runs.

## `services.ferm.config` {#opt-services.ferm.config}

Verbatim ferm.conf configuration.

## `services.ferm.enable` {#opt-services.ferm.enable}

Whether to enable Ferm Firewall.
*Warning*: Enabling this service WILL disable the existing NixOS

The ferm package to use.

*Type:* package

*Default:* `pkgs.ferm`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/ferm.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/ferm.nix)

## `services.ferretdb.enable` {#opt-services.ferretdb.enable}

Whether to enable FerretDB, an Open Source MongoDB alternative.

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/databases/ferretdb.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/databases/ferretdb.nix)

## `services.ferretdb.package` {#opt-services.ferretdb.package}

The ferretdb package to use.

*Type:* package

*Default:* `pkgs.ferretdb`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/databases/ferretdb.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/databases/ferretdb.nix)

## `services.ferretdb.settings` {#opt-services.ferretdb.settings}

Additional configuration for FerretDB, see

for supported values.

*Type:* open submodule of attribute set of string

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  FERRETDB_LOG_LEVEL = "warn";
  FERRETDB_MODE = "normal";
}
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/databases/ferretdb.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/databases/ferretdb.nix)

## `services.ferretdb.settings.FERRETDB_HANDLER` {#opt-services.ferretdb.settings.FERRETDB_HANDLER}

Backend handler

*Type:* one of "sqlite", "pg"

*Default:* `"sqlite"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/databases/ferretdb.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/databases/ferretdb.nix)

## `services.ferretdb.settings.FERRETDB_POSTGRESQL_URL` {#opt-services.ferretdb.settings.FERRETDB_POSTGRESQL_URL}

PostgreSQL URL for 'pg' handler

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"postgres://ferretdb@localhost/ferretdb?host=/run/postgresql"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/databases/ferretdb.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/databases/ferretdb.nix)

## `services.ferretdb.settings.FERRETDB_SQLITE_URL` {#opt-services.ferretdb.settings.FERRETDB_SQLITE_URL}

SQLite URI (directory) for 'sqlite' handler

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"file:/var/lib/ferretdb/"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/databases/ferretdb.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/databases/ferretdb.nix)

## `services.ferretdb.settings.FERRETDB_TELEMETRY` {#opt-services.ferretdb.settings.FERRETDB_TELEMETRY}

Enable or disable basic telemetry.

See [https://docs.ferretdb.io/telemetry/](https://docs.ferretdb.io/telemetry/) for more information.

*Type:* one of "enable", "disable"

*Default:* `"disable"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/databases/ferretdb.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/databases/ferretdb.nix)

## `services.fider.dataDir` {#opt-services.fider.dataDir}

Default data folder for Fider.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"/var/lib/fider"`

*Example:* `"/mnt/fider"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fider.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fider.nix)

## `services.fider.database.url` {#opt-services.fider.database.url}

URI to use for the main PostgreSQL database. If this needs to include
credentials that shouldn't be world-readable in the Nix store, set an
environment file on the systemd service and override the
`DATABASE_URL` entry. Pass the string
`local` to setup a database on the local server.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"local"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fider.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fider.nix)

## `services.fider.enable` {#opt-services.fider.enable}

Whether to enable the Fider server.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fider.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fider.nix)

## `services.fider.environment` {#opt-services.fider.environment}

Environment variables to set for the service. Secrets should be
specified using `environmentFiles`.
Refer to [https://github.com/getfider/fider/blob/stable/.example.env](https://github.com/getfider/fider/blob/stable/.example.env)
and [https://github.com/getfider/fider/blob/stable/app/pkg/env/env.go](https://github.com/getfider/fider/blob/stable/app/pkg/env/env.go)
for available options.

*Type:* attribute set of string

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  BASE_URL = "https://fider.example.com";
  BLOB_STORAGE = "fs";
  EMAIL = "smtp";
  EMAIL_NOREPLY = "fider@example.com";
  EMAIL_SMTP_HOST = "mail.example.com";
  EMAIL_SMTP_PORT = "587";
  EMAIL_SMTP_USERNAME = "fider@example.com";
  PORT = "31213";
}
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fider.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fider.nix)

## `services.fider.environmentFiles` {#opt-services.fider.environmentFiles}

Files to load environment variables from. Loaded variables override
values set in `environment`.

*Type:* list of absolute path

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:* `"/run/secrets/fider.env"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fider.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fider.nix)

## `services.fider.package` {#opt-services.fider.package}

The fider package to use.

*Type:* package

*Default:* `pkgs.fider`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fider.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fider.nix)

## `services.filebeat.enable` {#opt-services.filebeat.enable}

Whether to enable filebeat.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/logging/filebeat.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/logging/filebeat.nix)

## `services.filebeat.inputs` {#opt-services.filebeat.inputs}

Inputs specify how Filebeat locates and processes input data.

This is like `services.filebeat.settings.filebeat.inputs`,
but structured as an attribute set. This has the benefit
that multiple NixOS modules can contribute settings to a
single filebeat input.

An input type can be specified multiple times by choosing a
different `<name>` for each, but setting
[services (F)](#opt-services.filebeat.inputs._name_.type)
to the same value.

See [https://www.elastic.co/guide/en/beats/filebeat/current/configuration-filebeat-options.html](https://www.elastic.co/guide/en/beats/filebeat/current/configuration-filebeat-options.html).

*Type:* attribute set of (open submodule of (JSON value))

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  journald.id = "everything";  # Only for filebeat7
  log = {
    enabled = true;
    paths = [
      "/var/log/*.log"
    ];
  };
};
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/logging/filebeat.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/logging/filebeat.nix)

## `services.filebeat.inputs.<name>.type` {#opt-services.filebeat.inputs._name_.type}

The input type.

Look for the value after `type:` on
the individual input pages linked from
[https://www.elastic.co/guide/en/beats/filebeat/current/configuration-filebeat-options.html](https://www.elastic.co/guide/en/beats/filebeat/current/configuration-filebeat-options.html).

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"‹name›"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/logging/filebeat.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/logging/filebeat.nix)

## `services.filebeat.modules` {#opt-services.filebeat.modules}

Filebeat modules provide a quick way to get started
processing common log formats. They contain default
configurations, Elasticsearch ingest pipeline definitions,
and Kibana dashboards to help you implement and deploy a log
monitoring solution.

This is like `services.filebeat.settings.filebeat.modules`,
but structured as an attribute set. This has the benefit
that multiple NixOS modules can contribute settings to a
single filebeat module.

A module can be specified multiple times by choosing a
different `<name>` for each, but setting
[services (F)](#opt-services.filebeat.modules._name_.module)
to the same value.

See [https://www.elastic.co/guide/en/beats/filebeat/current/filebeat-modules.html](https://www.elastic.co/guide/en/beats/filebeat/current/filebeat-modules.html).

*Type:* attribute set of (open submodule of (JSON value))

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  nginx = {
    access = {
      enabled = true;
      var.paths = [ "/path/to/log/nginx/access.log*" ];
    };
    error = {
      enabled = true;
      var.paths = [ "/path/to/log/nginx/error.log*" ];
    };
  };
};
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/logging/filebeat.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/logging/filebeat.nix)

## `services.filebeat.modules.<name>.module` {#opt-services.filebeat.modules._name_.module}

The name of the module.

Look for the value after `module:` on
the individual input pages linked from
[https://www.elastic.co/guide/en/beats/filebeat/current/filebeat-modules.html](https://www.elastic.co/guide/en/beats/filebeat/current/filebeat-modules.html).

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"‹name›"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/logging/filebeat.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/logging/filebeat.nix)

## `services.filebeat.package` {#opt-services.filebeat.package}

The filebeat package to use.

*Type:* package

*Default:* `pkgs.filebeat`

*Example:* `filebeat7`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/logging/filebeat.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/logging/filebeat.nix)

## `services.filebeat.settings` {#opt-services.filebeat.settings}

Configuration for filebeat. See
[https://www.elastic.co/guide/en/beats/filebeat/current/filebeat-reference-yml.html](https://www.elastic.co/guide/en/beats/filebeat/current/filebeat-reference-yml.html)
for supported values.

Options containing secret data should be set to an attribute
set containing the attribute `_secret` - a
string pointing to a file containing the value the option
should be set to. See the example to get a better picture of
this: in the resulting
`filebeat.yml` file, the
`output.elasticsearch.password`
key will be set to the contents of the
`/var/keys/elasticsearch_password` file.

*Type:* open submodule of (JSON value)

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  settings = {
    output.elasticsearch = {
      hosts = [ "myEShost:9200" ];
      username = "filebeat_internal";
      password = { _secret = "/var/keys/elasticsearch_password"; };
    };
    logging.level = "info";
  };
};
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/logging/filebeat.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/logging/filebeat.nix)

## `services.filebeat.settings.output.elasticsearch.hosts` {#opt-services.filebeat.settings.output.elasticsearch.hosts}

The list of Elasticsearch nodes to connect to.

The events are distributed to these nodes in round
robin order. If one node becomes unreachable, the
event is automatically sent to another node. Each
Elasticsearch node can be defined as a URL or
IP:PORT. For example:
`http://192.15.3.2`,
`https://es.found.io:9230` or
`192.24.3.2:9300`. If no port is
specified, `9200` is used.

*Type:* list of string

*Default:*

``` nix
[
  "127.0.0.1:9200"
]
```

*Example:*

``` nix
[
  "myEShost:9200"
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/logging/filebeat.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/logging/filebeat.nix)
