## `services.filesender.poolSettings` {#opt-services.filesender.poolSettings}

Options for FileSender's PHP pool.

See the documentation on `php-fpm.conf` for details on configuration
directives.

*Type:* attribute set of (string or signed integer or boolean)

*Default:*

``` nix
{
  pm = "dynamic";
  "pm.max_children" = "32";
  "pm.max_requests" = "500";
  "pm.max_spare_servers" = "4";
  "pm.min_spare_servers" = "2";
  "pm.start_servers" = "2";
}
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/filesender.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/filesender.nix)

## `services.filesender.settings` {#opt-services.filesender.settings}

Configuration options used by FileSender.

See [](https://docs.filesender.org/filesender/v3.0/admin/configuration/)
for available options.

*Type:* open submodule of (PHP value)

*Default:* `{ }`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/filesender.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/filesender.nix)

## `services.filesender.settings.admin` {#opt-services.filesender.settings.admin}

UIDs (as per the configured saml\_uid\_attribute) of FileSender administrators.
Accounts with these UIDs can access the Admin page through the web UI.

*Type:* strings concatenated with ","

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/filesender.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/filesender.nix)

## `services.filesender.settings.admin_email` {#opt-services.filesender.settings.admin_email}

Email address of FileSender administrator(s).
Emails regarding disk full etc. are sent here.
You should use a role-address here.

*Type:* strings concatenated with ","

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/filesender.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/filesender.nix)

## `services.filesender.settings.log_facilities` {#opt-services.filesender.settings.log_facilities}

Defines where FileSender logging is sent.

You can sent logging to a file, to syslog or to the default PHP
log facility (as configured through your webserver's PHP module).

The directive takes an array of one or more logging targets.
Logging can be sent to multiple targets simultaneously, each a
list containing the name of the logging target and a number of
attributes which vary per log target.

*Type:* PHP value

*Default:*

``` nix
[
  {
    type = "error_log";
  }
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/filesender.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/filesender.nix)

## `services.filesender.settings.site_url` {#opt-services.filesender.settings.site_url}

Site URL. Used in emails, to build URLs for logging in, logging
out, build URL for upload endpoint for web workers, to include
scripts etc.

*Type:* string

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/filesender.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/filesender.nix)

## `services.filesender.settings.storage_filesystem_path` {#opt-services.filesender.settings.storage_filesystem_path}

FileSender storage root.

When using a storage-type filesystem, this is the absolute path
to the file system where uploaded files are stored until they
expire.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/filesender.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/filesender.nix)

## `services.filesender.user` {#opt-services.filesender.user}

User under which filesender runs.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"filesender"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/filesender.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/filesender.nix)

## `services.firebird.baseDir` {#opt-services.firebird.baseDir}

Location containing data/ and system/ directories.
data/ stores the databases, system/ stores the password database security2.fdb.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"/var/lib/firebird"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/databases/firebird.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/databases/firebird.nix)

## `services.firebird.enable` {#opt-services.firebird.enable}

Whether to enable the Firebird super server.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/databases/firebird.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/databases/firebird.nix)

## `services.firebird.package` {#opt-services.firebird.package}

The firebird package to use. For SuperServer use override: `pkgs.firebird_3.override { superServer = true; };`

*Type:* package

*Default:* `pkgs.firebird`

*Example:* `firebird_3`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/databases/firebird.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/databases/firebird.nix)

## `services.firebird.port` {#opt-services.firebird.port}

Port Firebird uses.

*Type:* 16 bit unsigned integer; between 0 and 65535 (both inclusive)

*Default:* `3050`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/databases/firebird.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/databases/firebird.nix)

## `services.firebird.user` {#opt-services.firebird.user}

User account under which firebird runs.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"firebird"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/databases/firebird.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/databases/firebird.nix)

## `services.firefly-iii-data-importer.dataDir` {#opt-services.firefly-iii-data-importer.dataDir}

The place where firefly-iii data importer stores its state.

*Type:* absolute path

*Default:* `"/var/lib/firefly-iii-data-importer"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii-data-importer.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii-data-importer.nix)

## `services.firefly-iii-data-importer.enable` {#opt-services.firefly-iii-data-importer.enable}

Whether to enable Firefly III Data Importer.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii-data-importer.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii-data-importer.nix)

## `services.firefly-iii-data-importer.enableNginx` {#opt-services.firefly-iii-data-importer.enableNginx}

Whether to enable nginx or not. If enabled, an nginx virtual host will
be created for access to firefly-iii data importer. If not enabled, then you may use
`${config.services.firefly-iii-data-importer.package}` as your document root in
whichever webserver you wish to setup.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii-data-importer.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii-data-importer.nix)

## `services.firefly-iii-data-importer.group` {#opt-services.firefly-iii-data-importer.group}

Group under which firefly-iii-data-importer runs.
While this also changes which group has access to the phpfpm socket,
changing `services.firefly-iii-data-importer.poolConfig."listen.group"`
is preferred.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"firefly-iii-data-importer"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii-data-importer.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii-data-importer.nix)

## `services.firefly-iii-data-importer.package` {#opt-services.firefly-iii-data-importer.package}

The firefly-iii-data-importer package served by php-fpm and the webserver of choice.
This option can be used to point the webserver to the correct root. It
may also be used to set the package to a different version, say a
development version.

*Type:* package

*Default:* `pkgs.firefly-iii-data-importer`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii-data-importer.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii-data-importer.nix)

## `services.firefly-iii-data-importer.poolConfig` {#opt-services.firefly-iii-data-importer.poolConfig}

Options for the Firefly III Data Importer PHP pool. See the documentation on <literal>php-fpm.conf</literal>
for details on configuration directives.

*Type:* attribute set of (string or signed integer or boolean)

*Default:*

``` nix
{
  "listen.mode" = "0660";
  "listen.owner" = if config.services.firefly-iii-data-importer.enableNginx then config.services.nginx.user else config.services.firefly-iii-data-importer.user;
  "listen.group" = if config.services.firefly-iii-data-importer.enableNginx then config.services.nginx.group else config.services.firefly-iii-data-importer.group;
  "pm" = "dynamic";
  "pm.max_children" = 32;
  "pm.start_servers" = 2;
  "pm.min_spare_servers" = 2;
  "pm.max_spare_servers" = 4;
  "pm.max_requests" = 500;
}
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii-data-importer.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii-data-importer.nix)

## `services.firefly-iii-data-importer.settings` {#opt-services.firefly-iii-data-importer.settings}

Options for firefly-iii data importer configuration. Refer to
[https://github.com/firefly-iii/data-importer/blob/main/.env.example](https://github.com/firefly-iii/data-importer/blob/main/.env.example) for
details on supported values. All <option>\_FILE values supported by
upstream are supported here.

APP\_URL will be the same as `services.firefly-iii-data-importer.virtualHost` if the
former is unset in `services.firefly-iii-data-importer.settings`.

*Type:* open submodule of attribute set of (string or signed integer or boolean)

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  APP_ENV = "local";
  LOG_CHANNEL = "syslog";
  FIREFLY_III_ACCESS_TOKEN= = "/var/secrets/firefly-iii-access-token.txt";
}
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii-data-importer.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii-data-importer.nix)

## `services.firefly-iii-data-importer.user` {#opt-services.firefly-iii-data-importer.user}

User account under which firefly-iii-data-importer runs.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"firefly-iii-data-importer"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii-data-importer.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii-data-importer.nix)

## `services.firefly-iii-data-importer.virtualHost` {#opt-services.firefly-iii-data-importer.virtualHost}

The hostname at which you wish firefly-iii-data-importer to be served. If you have
enabled nginx using `services.firefly-iii-data-importer.enableNginx` then this will
be used.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"localhost"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii-data-importer.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii-data-importer.nix)

## `services.firefly-iii.dataDir` {#opt-services.firefly-iii.dataDir}

The place where firefly-iii stores its state.

*Type:* absolute path

*Default:* `"/var/lib/firefly-iii"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii.nix)

## `services.firefly-iii.enable` {#opt-services.firefly-iii.enable}

Whether to enable Firefly III: A free and open source personal finance manager.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii.nix)

## `services.firefly-iii.enableNginx` {#opt-services.firefly-iii.enableNginx}

Whether to enable nginx or not. If enabled, an nginx virtual host will
be created for access to firefly-iii. If not enabled, then you may use
`${config.services.firefly-iii.package}` as your document root in
whichever webserver you wish to setup.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii.nix)

## `services.firefly-iii.group` {#opt-services.firefly-iii.group}

Group under which firefly-iii runs.
While this also changes which group has access to the phpfpm socket,
changing `services.firefly-iii.poolConfig."listen.group"`
is preferred.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"firefly-iii"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii.nix)

## `services.firefly-iii.package` {#opt-services.firefly-iii.package}

The firefly-iii package to use.

*Type:* package

*Default:* `pkgs.firefly-iii`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii.nix)

## `services.firefly-iii.poolConfig` {#opt-services.firefly-iii.poolConfig}

Options for the Firefly III PHP pool. See the documentation on <literal>php-fpm.conf</literal>
for details on configuration directives.

*Type:* attribute set of (string or signed integer or boolean)

*Default:*

``` nix
''
  {
    "listen.mode" = "0660";
    "listen.owner" = if config.services.firefly-iii.enableNginx then config.services.nginx.user else config.services.firefly-iii.user;
    "listen.group" = if config.services.firefly-iii.enableNginx then config.services.nginx.group else config.services.firefly-iii.group;
    "pm" = "dynamic";
    "pm.max_children" = 32;
    "pm.start_servers" = 2;
    "pm.min_spare_servers" = 2;
    "pm.max_spare_servers" = 4;
    "pm.max_requests" = 500;
  }
''
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii.nix)

## `services.firefly-iii.settings` {#opt-services.firefly-iii.settings}

Options for firefly-iii configuration. Refer to
[https://github.com/firefly-iii/firefly-iii/blob/main/.env.example](https://github.com/firefly-iii/firefly-iii/blob/main/.env.example) for
details on supported values. All <option>\_FILE values supported by
upstream are supported here.

APP\_URL will be the same as `services.firefly-iii.virtualHost` if the
former is unset in `services.firefly-iii.settings`.

*Type:* open submodule of attribute set of (string or signed integer or boolean)

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  APP_ENV = "production";
  APP_KEY_FILE = "/var/secrets/firefly-iii-app-key.txt";
  SITE_OWNER = "mail@example.com";
  DB_CONNECTION = "mysql";
  DB_HOST = "db";
  DB_PORT = 3306;
  DB_DATABASE = "firefly";
  DB_USERNAME = "firefly";
  DB_PASSWORD_FILE = "/var/secrets/firefly-iii-mysql-password.txt";
}
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii.nix)

## `services.firefly-iii.settings.APP_ENV` {#opt-services.firefly-iii.settings.APP_ENV}

The app environment. It is recommended to keep this at "local".
Possible values are "local", "production" and "testing"

*Type:* one of "local", "production", "testing"

*Default:* `"local"`

*Example:* `"production"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii.nix)

## `services.firefly-iii.settings.APP_KEY_FILE` {#opt-services.firefly-iii.settings.APP_KEY_FILE}

The path to your appkey. The file should contain a 32 character
random app key. This may be set using `echo "base64:$(head -c 32 /dev/urandom | base64)" > /path/to/key-file`.

*Type:* absolute path

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii.nix)

## `services.firefly-iii.settings.APP_URL` {#opt-services.firefly-iii.settings.APP_URL}

The APP\_URL used by firefly-iii internally. Please make sure this
URL matches the external URL of your Firefly III installation. It
is used to validate specific requests and to generate URLs in
emails.

*Type:* string

*Default:*

``` nix
''
  http(s)://''${config.services.firefly-iii.virtualHost}
''
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii.nix)

## `services.firefly-iii.settings.DB_CONNECTION` {#opt-services.firefly-iii.settings.DB_CONNECTION}

The type of database you wish to use. Can be one of "sqlite",
"mysql" or "pgsql".

*Type:* one of "sqlite", "pgsql", "mysql"

*Default:* `"sqlite"`

*Example:* `"pgsql"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii.nix)

## `services.firefly-iii.settings.DB_HOST` {#opt-services.firefly-iii.settings.DB_HOST}

The machine which hosts your database. This is left at the
default value for "mysql" because we use the "DB\_SOCKET" option
to connect to a unix socket instead. "pgsql" requires that the
unix socket location be specified here instead of at "DB\_SOCKET".
This option does not affect "sqlite".

*Type:* string

*Default:*

``` nix
''
  "localhost" if DB_CONNECTION is "sqlite" or "mysql", "/run/postgresql" if "pgsql".
''
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii.nix)

## `services.firefly-iii.settings.DB_PORT` {#opt-services.firefly-iii.settings.DB_PORT}

The port your database is listening at. sqlite does not require
this value to be filled.

*Type:* null or signed integer

*Default:*

``` nix
''
  `null` if DB_CONNECTION is "sqlite", `3306` if "mysql", `5432` if "pgsql"
''
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii.nix)

## `services.firefly-iii.user` {#opt-services.firefly-iii.user}

User account under which firefly-iii runs.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"firefly-iii"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii.nix)

## `services.firefly-iii.virtualHost` {#opt-services.firefly-iii.virtualHost}

The hostname at which you wish firefly-iii to be served. If you have
enabled nginx using `services.firefly-iii.enableNginx` then this will
be used.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"localhost"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/firefly-iii.nix)

## `services.firefox-syncserver.database.createLocally` {#opt-services.firefox-syncserver.database.createLocally}

Whether to create database and user on the local machine if they do not exist.
This includes enabling the configured database service and setting up
authentication for the configured user.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix)

## `services.firefox-syncserver.database.host` {#opt-services.firefox-syncserver.database.host}

Database host name. `localhost` is treated specially and inserts
systemd dependencies, other hostnames or IP addresses of the local machine do not.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"localhost"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix)

## `services.firefox-syncserver.database.name` {#opt-services.firefox-syncserver.database.name}

Database to use for storage. Will be created automatically if it does not exist
and `config.services.firefox-syncserver.database.createLocally` is set.

*Type:* string matching the pattern \[a-z\_\]\[a-z0-9\_\]\*

*Default:* `"firefox_syncserver"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix)

## `services.firefox-syncserver.database.type` {#opt-services.firefox-syncserver.database.type}

Which database backend to use for storage.

::: note
`"postgresql"` is recommended for new deployments. `"mysql"` is the
backend used by legacy installations.
:::

*Type:* one of "mysql", "postgresql"

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix)

## `services.firefox-syncserver.database.user` {#opt-services.firefox-syncserver.database.user}

Username for database connections.  When using PostgreSQL with
`createLocally`, this defaults to the database name so that
`ensureDBOwnership` works (it requires user and database names
to match).

*Type:* string

*Default:*

``` nix
if database.type == "postgresql" then "firefox_syncserver" else "firefox-syncserver"
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix)

## `services.firefox-syncserver.enable` {#opt-services.firefox-syncserver.enable}

Whether to enable the Firefox Sync storage service.

Out of the box this will not be very useful unless you also configure at least
one service and one nodes by inserting them into the database manually, e.g.
by running

```
  INSERT INTO services (id, service, pattern) VALUES (1, 'sync-1.5', '{node}/1.5/{uid}');
  INSERT INTO nodes (id, service, node, available, current_load,
      capacity, downed, backoff)
    VALUES (1, 1, 'https://mydomain.tld', 1, 0, 10, 0, 0);
```

[`services.firefox-syncserver.singleNode.enable`](#opt-services.firefox-syncserver.singleNode.enable) does this automatically when enabled
.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix)

## `services.firefox-syncserver.logLevel` {#opt-services.firefox-syncserver.logLevel}

Log level to run with. This can be a simple log level like `error`
or `trace`, or a more complicated logging expression.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"error"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix)

## `services.firefox-syncserver.package` {#opt-services.firefox-syncserver.package}

Syncstorage server package to use. When `null`, the package is
selected automatically based on [`services.firefox-syncserver.database.type`](#opt-services.firefox-syncserver.database.type).

*Type:* null or package

*Default:*

``` nix
if config.services.firefox-syncserver.database.type == "mysql" then
  pkgs.syncstorage-rs-mysql
else
  pkgs.syncstorage-rs-pgsql
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix)

## `services.firefox-syncserver.secrets` {#opt-services.firefox-syncserver.secrets}

A file containing the various secrets. Should be in the format expected by systemd's
`EnvironmentFile` directory. Two secrets are currently available:
`SYNC_MASTER_SECRET` and
`SYNC_TOKENSERVER__FXA_METRICS_HASH_SECRET`.

*Type:* absolute path

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix)

## `services.firefox-syncserver.settings` {#opt-services.firefox-syncserver.settings}

Settings for the sync server. These take priority over values computed
from NixOS options.

See the example config in
[https://github.com/mozilla-services/syncstorage-rs/blob/master/config/local.example.toml](https://github.com/mozilla-services/syncstorage-rs/blob/master/config/local.example.toml)
and the doc comments on the `Settings` structs in
[https://github.com/mozilla-services/syncstorage-rs/blob/master/syncstorage-settings/src/lib.rs](https://github.com/mozilla-services/syncstorage-rs/blob/master/syncstorage-settings/src/lib.rs)
and
[https://github.com/mozilla-services/syncstorage-rs/blob/master/tokenserver-settings/src/lib.rs](https://github.com/mozilla-services/syncstorage-rs/blob/master/tokenserver-settings/src/lib.rs)
for available options.

*Type:* open submodule of (TOML value)

*Default:* `{ }`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix)

## `services.firefox-syncserver.settings.port` {#opt-services.firefox-syncserver.settings.port}

Port to bind to.

*Type:* 16 bit unsigned integer; between 0 and 65535 (both inclusive)

*Default:* `5000`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix)

## `services.firefox-syncserver.settings.tokenserver.enabled` {#opt-services.firefox-syncserver.settings.tokenserver.enabled}

Whether to enable the token service as well.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix)

## `services.firefox-syncserver.singleNode.capacity` {#opt-services.firefox-syncserver.singleNode.capacity}

How many sync accounts are allowed on this server. Setting this value
equal to or less than the number of currently active accounts will
effectively deny service to accounts not yet registered here.

*Type:* unsigned integer, meaning \>=0

*Default:* `10`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix)

## `services.firefox-syncserver.singleNode.enable` {#opt-services.firefox-syncserver.singleNode.enable}

Whether to enable auto-configuration for a simple single-node setup.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix)

## `services.firefox-syncserver.singleNode.enableNginx` {#opt-services.firefox-syncserver.singleNode.enableNginx}

Whether to enable nginx virtualhost definitions.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix)

## `services.firefox-syncserver.singleNode.enableTLS` {#opt-services.firefox-syncserver.singleNode.enableTLS}

Whether to enable automatic TLS setup.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix)

## `services.firefox-syncserver.singleNode.hostname` {#opt-services.firefox-syncserver.singleNode.hostname}

Host name to use for this service.

*Type:* string

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix)

## `services.firefox-syncserver.singleNode.url` {#opt-services.firefox-syncserver.singleNode.url}

URL of the host. If you are not using the automatic webserver proxy setup you will have
to change this setting or your sync server may not be functional.

*Type:* string

*Default:*

``` nix
${if cfg.singleNode.enableTLS then "https" else "http"}://${config.services.firefox-syncserver.singleNode.hostname}
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firefox-syncserver.nix)

## `services.fireqos.config` {#opt-services.fireqos.config}

The FireQOS configuration.

*Type:* strings concatenated with "\\n"

*Example:*

``` nix
''
  interface wlp3s0 world-in input rate 10mbit ethernet
    class web commit 50kbit
      match tcp ports 80,443
  
  interface wlp3s0 world-out input rate 10mbit ethernet
    class web commit 50kbit
      match tcp ports 80,443
''
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fireqos.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fireqos.nix)

## `services.fireqos.enable` {#opt-services.fireqos.enable}

Whether to enable FireQOS.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/fireqos.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/fireqos.nix)

## `services.firewalld.enable` {#opt-services.firewalld.enable}

Whether to enable FirewallD.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld)

## `services.firewalld.extraArgs` {#opt-services.firewalld.extraArgs}

Extra arguments to pass to FirewallD.

*Type:* list of string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:*

``` nix
[
  "--debug"
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld)

## `services.firewalld.package` {#opt-services.firewalld.package}

The firewalld package to use.

*Type:* package

*Default:* `pkgs.firewalld`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld)

## `services.firewalld.packages` {#opt-services.firewalld.packages}

Packages providing firewalld zones and other files.
Files found in `/lib/firewalld` will be included.

*Type:* list of package

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld)

## `services.firewalld.services` {#opt-services.firewalld.services}

firewalld service configuration files. See `firewalld.service(5)`.

*Type:* attribute set of (submodule)

*Default:* `{ }`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/service.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/service.nix)

## `services.firewalld.services.<name>.description` {#opt-services.firewalld.services._name_.description}

Description for the service.

*Type:* null or non-empty string

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/service.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/service.nix)

## `services.firewalld.services.<name>.destination` {#opt-services.firewalld.services._name_.destination}

Destinations for the service.

*Type:* submodule

*Default:* `{ }`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/service.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/service.nix)

## `services.firewalld.services.<name>.destination.ipv4` {#opt-services.firewalld.services._name_.destination.ipv4}

IPv4 destination.

*Type:* null or string matching the pattern (\[0-9\]{1,3}.){3}\[0-9\]{1,3}(/\[0-9\]{1,2})?

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/service.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/service.nix)

## `services.firewalld.services.<name>.destination.ipv6` {#opt-services.firewalld.services._name_.destination.ipv6}

IPv6 destination.

*Type:* null or string matching the pattern \[0-9A-Fa-f:\]{3,39}(/\[0-9\]{1,3})?

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/service.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/service.nix)

## `services.firewalld.services.<name>.helpers` {#opt-services.firewalld.services._name_.helpers}

Helpers for the service.

*Type:* list of non-empty string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/service.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/service.nix)

## `services.firewalld.services.<name>.includes` {#opt-services.firewalld.services._name_.includes}

Services to include for the service.

*Type:* list of non-empty string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/service.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/service.nix)

## `services.firewalld.services.<name>.ports` {#opt-services.firewalld.services._name_.ports}

Ports of the service.

*Type:* list of (submodule)

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/service.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/service.nix)

## `services.firewalld.services.<name>.ports.*.port` {#opt-services.firewalld.services._name_.ports._.port}

*Type:* 16 bit unsigned integer; between 0 and 65535 (both inclusive) or (submodule)

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/service.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/service.nix)

## `services.firewalld.services.<name>.ports.*.protocol` {#opt-services.firewalld.services._name_.ports._.protocol}

*Type:* one of "tcp", "udp", "sctp", "dccp"

Version of the service.

*Type:* null or non-empty string

## `services.firewalld.settings` {#opt-services.firewalld.settings}

FirewallD config file.
See `firewalld.conf(5)`.

*Type:* open submodule of attribute set of (boolean or non-empty string)

*Default:* `{ }`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/settings.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/settings.nix)

## `services.firewalld.settings.CleanupModulesOnExit` {#opt-services.firewalld.settings.CleanupModulesOnExit}

Whether to unload all firewall-related kernel modules when firewalld stops.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/settings.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/settings.nix)

## `services.firewalld.settings.CleanupOnExit` {#opt-services.firewalld.settings.CleanupOnExit}

Whether to clean up firewall rules when firewalld stops.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/settings.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/settings.nix)

## `services.firewalld.settings.DefaultZone` {#opt-services.firewalld.settings.DefaultZone}

Default zone for connections.

*Type:* non-empty string

*Default:* `"public"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/settings.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/settings.nix)

## `services.firewalld.settings.FirewallBackend` {#opt-services.firewalld.settings.FirewallBackend}

The firewall backend implementation.
This applies to all firewalld primitives.
The only exception is direct and passthrough rules which always use the traditional iptables, ip6tables, and ebtables backends.

::: caution
The iptables backend is deprecated.
It will be removed in a future release.
:::

*Type:* one of "nftables", "iptables"

*Default:* `"nftables"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/settings.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/settings.nix)

## `services.firewalld.settings.FlushAllOnReload` {#opt-services.firewalld.settings.FlushAllOnReload}

Whether to flush all runtime rules on a reload.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/settings.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/settings.nix)

## `services.firewalld.settings.IPv6_rpfilter` {#opt-services.firewalld.settings.IPv6_rpfilter}

Performs reverse path filtering (RPF) on IPv6 packets as per RFC 3704.

Possible values:

`"strict"`
: Performs "strict" filtering as per RFC 3704.
This check verifies that the in ingress interface is the same interface that would be used to send a packet reply to the source.
That is, `ingress == egress`.

`"loose"`
: Performs "loose" filtering as per RFC 3704.
This check only verifies that there is a route back to the source through any interface; even if it's not the same one on which the packet arrived.

`"strict-forward"`
: This is almost identical to "strict", but does not perform RPF for packets targeted to the host (INPUT).

`"loose-forward"`
: This is almost identical to "loose", but does not perform RPF for packets targeted to the host (INPUT).

`"no"`
: RPF is completely disabled.

The rp\_filter for IPv4 is controlled using sysctl.

*Type:* one of "strict", "loose", "strict-forward", "loose-forward", "no"

*Default:* `"strict"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/settings.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/settings.nix)

## `services.firewalld.settings.IndividualCalls` {#opt-services.firewalld.settings.IndividualCalls}

Whether to use individual -restore calls to apply changes to the firewall.
The use of individual calls increases the time that is needed to apply changes and to start the daemon, but is good for debugging as error messages are more specific.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/settings.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/settings.nix)

## `services.firewalld.settings.LogDenied` {#opt-services.firewalld.settings.LogDenied}

Add logging rules right before reject and drop rules in the INPUT, FORWARD and OUTPUT chains for the default rules and also final reject and drop rules in zones for the configured link-layer packet type.

*Type:* one of "all", "unicast", "broadcast", "multicast", "off"

*Default:* `"off"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/settings.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/settings.nix)

## `services.firewalld.settings.NftablesCounters` {#opt-services.firewalld.settings.NftablesCounters}

Whether to add a counter to every nftables rule.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/settings.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/settings.nix)

## `services.firewalld.settings.NftablesFlowtable` {#opt-services.firewalld.settings.NftablesFlowtable}

This may improve forwarded traffic throughput by enabling nftables flowtable.
It is a software fastpath and avoids calling nftables rule evaluation for data packets.
Its value is a space separate list of interfaces.

*Type:* strings concatenated with " "

*Default:* `"off"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/settings.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/settings.nix)

## `services.firewalld.settings.NftablesTableOwner` {#opt-services.firewalld.settings.NftablesTableOwner}

If enabled, the generated nftables rule set will be owned exclusively by firewalld.
This prevents other entities from mistakenly (or maliciously) modifying firewalld's rule set.
If you intend to modify firewalld's rules, set this to `false`.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/settings.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/settings.nix)

## `services.firewalld.settings.RFC3964_IPv4` {#opt-services.firewalld.settings.RFC3964_IPv4}

Whether to filter IPv6 traffic with 6to4 destination addresses that correspond to IPv4 addresses that should not be routed over the public internet.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/settings.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/settings.nix)

## `services.firewalld.settings.ReloadPolicy` {#opt-services.firewalld.settings.ReloadPolicy}

The policy during reload.

*Type:* one of "DROP", "REJECT", "ACCEPT" or strings concatenated with ","

*Default:* `"INPUT:DROP,FORWARD:DROP,OUTPUT:DROP"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/settings.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/settings.nix)

## `services.firewalld.settings.StrictForwardPorts` {#opt-services.firewalld.settings.StrictForwardPorts}

If enabled, the generated destination NAT (DNAT) rules will NOT accept traffic that was DNAT'd by other entities, e.g. docker.
Firewalld will be strict and not allow published container ports until they're explicitly allowed via firewalld.
If set to `false`, then docker (and podman) integrates seamlessly with firewalld.
Published container ports are implicitly allowed.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/settings.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/settings.nix)

## `services.firewalld.zones` {#opt-services.firewalld.zones}

firewalld zone configuration files.
See `firewalld.zone(5)`.

*Type:* attribute set of (submodule)

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  dmz = {
    forward = true;
    services = [
      "ssh"
    ];
  };
  external = {
    forward = true;
    masquerade = true;
    services = [
      "ssh"
    ];
  };
  home = {
    forward = true;
    services = [
      "ssh"
      "mdns"
      "samba-client"
      "dhcpv6-client"
    ];
  };
  internal = {
    forward = true;
    services = [
      "ssh"
      "mdns"
      "samba-client"
      "dhcpv6-client"
    ];
  };
  public = {
    forward = true;
    services = [
      "ssh"
      "dhcpv6-client"
    ];
  };
  work = {
    forward = true;
    services = [
      "ssh"
      "dhcpv6-client"
    ];
  };
}
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix)

## `services.firewalld.zones.<name>.description` {#opt-services.firewalld.zones._name_.description}

Description for the zone.

*Type:* null or non-empty string

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix)

## `services.firewalld.zones.<name>.egressPriority` {#opt-services.firewalld.zones._name_.egressPriority}

Priority for outbound traffic.
Lower values have higher priority.

*Type:* null or 16 bit signed integer; between -32768 and 32767 (both inclusive)

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix)

## `services.firewalld.zones.<name>.forward` {#opt-services.firewalld.zones._name_.forward}

Whether to enable intra-zone forwarding.
When enabled, packets will be forwarded between interfaces or sources within a zone, even if the zone's target is not set to ACCEPT.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix)

## `services.firewalld.zones.<name>.forwardPorts` {#opt-services.firewalld.zones._name_.forwardPorts}

Ports to forward in the zone.

*Type:* list of (submodule)

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix)

## `services.firewalld.zones.<name>.forwardPorts.*.port` {#opt-services.firewalld.zones._name_.forwardPorts._.port}

*Type:* 16 bit unsigned integer; between 0 and 65535 (both inclusive) or (submodule)

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix)

## `services.firewalld.zones.<name>.forwardPorts.*.protocol` {#opt-services.firewalld.zones._name_.forwardPorts._.protocol}

*Type:* one of "tcp", "udp", "sctp", "dccp"

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix)

## `services.firewalld.zones.<name>.forwardPorts.*.to-addr` {#opt-services.firewalld.zones._name_.forwardPorts._.to-addr}

Destination IP address.

*Type:* null or non-empty string

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix)

## `services.firewalld.zones.<name>.forwardPorts.*.to-port` {#opt-services.firewalld.zones._name_.forwardPorts._.to-port}

*Type:* null or 16 bit unsigned integer; between 0 and 65535 (both inclusive) or (submodule)

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix)

## `services.firewalld.zones.<name>.icmpBlockInversion` {#opt-services.firewalld.zones._name_.icmpBlockInversion}

Whether to invert the icmp block handling.
Only enabled ICMP types are accepted and all others are rejected in the zone.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix)

## `services.firewalld.zones.<name>.icmpBlocks` {#opt-services.firewalld.zones._name_.icmpBlocks}

ICMP types to block in the zone.

*Type:* list of non-empty string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix)

## `services.firewalld.zones.<name>.ingressPriority` {#opt-services.firewalld.zones._name_.ingressPriority}

Priority for inbound traffic.
Lower values have higher priority.

*Type:* null or 16 bit signed integer; between -32768 and 32767 (both inclusive)

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix)

## `services.firewalld.zones.<name>.interfaces` {#opt-services.firewalld.zones._name_.interfaces}

Interfaces to bind.

*Type:* list of non-empty string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix)

## `services.firewalld.zones.<name>.masquerade` {#opt-services.firewalld.zones._name_.masquerade}

Whether to enable masquerading in the zone.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix)

## `services.firewalld.zones.<name>.ports` {#opt-services.firewalld.zones._name_.ports}

Ports to allow in the zone.

*Type:* list of (submodule)

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix)

## `services.firewalld.zones.<name>.ports.*.port` {#opt-services.firewalld.zones._name_.ports._.port}

*Type:* 16 bit unsigned integer; between 0 and 65535 (both inclusive) or (submodule)

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix)

## `services.firewalld.zones.<name>.ports.*.protocol` {#opt-services.firewalld.zones._name_.ports._.protocol}

*Type:* one of "tcp", "udp", "sctp", "dccp"

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix)

## `services.firewalld.zones.<name>.protocols` {#opt-services.firewalld.zones._name_.protocols}

Protocols to allow in the zone.

*Type:* list of non-empty string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix)

## `services.firewalld.zones.<name>.rules` {#opt-services.firewalld.zones._name_.rules}

Rich rules for the zone.

*Type:* list of XML value

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix)

## `services.firewalld.zones.<name>.services` {#opt-services.firewalld.zones._name_.services}

Services to allow in the zone.

*Type:* list of non-empty string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix)

## `services.firewalld.zones.<name>.short` {#opt-services.firewalld.zones._name_.short}

Short description for the zone.

*Type:* null or non-empty string

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix)

## `services.firewalld.zones.<name>.sourcePorts` {#opt-services.firewalld.zones._name_.sourcePorts}

Source ports to allow in the zone.

*Type:* list of (submodule)

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix)

## `services.firewalld.zones.<name>.sourcePorts.*.port` {#opt-services.firewalld.zones._name_.sourcePorts._.port}

*Type:* 16 bit unsigned integer; between 0 and 65535 (both inclusive) or (submodule)

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix)

## `services.firewalld.zones.<name>.sourcePorts.*.protocol` {#opt-services.firewalld.zones._name_.sourcePorts._.protocol}

*Type:* one of "tcp", "udp", "sctp", "dccp"

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix)

## `services.firewalld.zones.<name>.sources` {#opt-services.firewalld.zones._name_.sources}

Source addresses, address ranges, MAC addresses or ipsets to bind.

*Type:* list of attribute-tagged union with choices: address, ipset, mac

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix)

## `services.firewalld.zones.<name>.sources.*.address` {#opt-services.firewalld.zones._name_.sources._.address}

An IP address or a network IP address with a mask for IPv4 or IPv6.
For IPv4, the mask can be a network mask or a plain number.
For IPv6 the mask is a plain number.
The use of host names is not supported.

*Type:* non-empty string

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix)

## `services.firewalld.zones.<name>.sources.*.ipset` {#opt-services.firewalld.zones._name_.sources._.ipset}

An ipset.

*Type:* non-empty string

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix)

## `services.firewalld.zones.<name>.sources.*.mac` {#opt-services.firewalld.zones._name_.sources._.mac}

A MAC address.

*Type:* string matching the pattern (\[\[:xdigit:\]\]{2}:){5}\[\[:xdigit:\]\]{2}

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix)

## `services.firewalld.zones.<name>.target` {#opt-services.firewalld.zones._name_.target}

Action for packets that doesn't match any rules.

*Type:* one of "ACCEPT", "%%REJECT%%", "DROP"

*Default:* `"%%REJECT%%"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix)

## `services.firewalld.zones.<name>.version` {#opt-services.firewalld.zones._name_.version}

Version of the zone.

*Type:* null or non-empty string

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firewalld/zone.nix)

## `services.firezone.gateway.apiUrl` {#opt-services.firezone.gateway.apiUrl}

The URL of your firezone server's API. This should be the same
as your server's setting for `services.firezone.server.settings.api.externalUrl`,
but with `wss://` instead of `https://`.

*Type:* string matching the pattern ^wss://.+/$

*Example:* `"wss://firezone.example.com/api/"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/gateway.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/gateway.nix)

## `services.firezone.gateway.enable` {#opt-services.firezone.gateway.enable}

Whether to enable the firezone gateway.

You have to manually masquerade and forward traffic from the
tun-firezone interface to your resource! Refer to the
[upstream setup script](https://github.com/firezone/firezone/blob/8c7c0a9e8e33ae790aeb75fdb5a15432c2870b79/scripts/gateway-systemd-install.sh#L154-L168)
for a list of iptable commands.

See the firezone nixos test in this repository for an nftables based example.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/gateway.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/gateway.nix)

## `services.firezone.gateway.enableTelemetry` {#opt-services.firezone.gateway.enableTelemetry}

Whether to enable telemetry.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

This file will be passed via systemd credentials, it should only be accessible

## `services.firezone.relay.apiUrl` {#opt-services.firezone.relay.apiUrl}

## `services.firezone.relay.highestPort` {#opt-services.firezone.relay.highestPort}

The highest port to use in TURN allocation

*Type:* 16 bit unsigned integer; between 0 and 65535 (both inclusive)

*Default:* `65535`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/relay.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/relay.nix)

The lowest port to use in TURN allocation

*Type:* 16 bit unsigned integer; between 0 and 65535 (both inclusive)

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/relay.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/relay.nix)

## `services.firezone.relay.openFirewall` {#opt-services.firezone.relay.openFirewall}

Opens up the main STUN port and the TURN allocation range.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/relay.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/relay.nix)

The firezone-relay package to use.

## `services.firezone.relay.port` {#opt-services.firezone.relay.port}

The port to listen on for STUN messages

*Type:* 16 bit unsigned integer; between 0 and 65535 (both inclusive)

*Default:* `3478`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/relay.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/relay.nix)

## `services.firezone.relay.publicIpv4` {#opt-services.firezone.relay.publicIpv4}
