The public ipv6 address of this relay

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/relay.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/relay.nix)

## `services.firezone.relay.tokenFile` {#opt-services.firezone.relay.tokenFile}

A file containing the firezone relay token. Do not use a nix-store path here
as it will make the token publicly readable!

This file will be passed via systemd credentials, it should only be accessible
by the root user.

*Type:* absolute path

*Example:* `"/run/secrets/firezone-relay-token"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/relay.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/relay.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.api.address` {#opt-services.firezone.server.api.address}

The address to listen on

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"127.0.0.1"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.api.enable` {#opt-services.firezone.server.api.enable}

Whether to enable the Firezone api server.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.api.externalUrl` {#opt-services.firezone.server.api.externalUrl}

The external URL under which you will serve the api. You need to
setup a reverse proxy for TLS termination, either with
[`services.firezone.server.nginx.enable`](#opt-services.firezone.server.nginx.enable) or manually.

*Type:* string matching the pattern ^https://.+/$

*Example:* `"https://firezone.example.com/api/"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.api.package` {#opt-services.firezone.server.api.package}

The firezone-server-api package to use.

*Type:* package

*Default:* `pkgs.firezone-server-api`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.api.port` {#opt-services.firezone.server.api.port}

The port under which the api will be served locally

*Type:* 16 bit unsigned integer; between 0 and 65535 (both inclusive)

*Default:* `8081`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.api.settings` {#opt-services.firezone.server.api.settings}

Environment variables for this component of the Firezone server. For a
list of available variables, please refer to the [upstream definitions](https://github.com/firezone/firezone/blob/main/elixir/apps/domain/lib/domain/config/definitions.ex).
Some variables like `OUTBOUND_EMAIL_ADAPTER_OPTS` require json values
for which you can use `VAR = builtins.toJSON { /* ... */ }`.

This component will automatically inherit all variables defined via
[`services.firezone.server.settings`](#opt-services.firezone.server.settings) and
[`services.firezone.server.settingsSecret`](#opt-services.firezone.server.settingsSecret), but which can be
overwritten by this option.

*Type:* open submodule of attribute set of (boolean or floating point number or signed integer or string or absolute path or package)

*Default:* `{ }`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.api.trustedProxies` {#opt-services.firezone.server.api.trustedProxies}

A list of trusted proxies

*Type:* list of string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.clusterHosts` {#opt-services.firezone.server.clusterHosts}

A list of components and their hosts that are part of this cluster. For
a single-machine setup, the default value will be sufficient. This
value will automatically set `ERLANG_CLUSTER_ADAPTER_CONFIG`.

The format is `<COMPONENT_NAME>@<HOSTNAME>`.

*Type:* list of string

*Default:*

``` nix
[
  "api@localhost.localdomain"
  "web@localhost.localdomain"
  "domain@localhost.localdomain"
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.domain.enable` {#opt-services.firezone.server.domain.enable}

Whether to enable the Firezone domain server.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.domain.package` {#opt-services.firezone.server.domain.package}

The firezone-server-domain package to use.

*Type:* package

*Default:* `pkgs.firezone-server-domain`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.domain.settings` {#opt-services.firezone.server.domain.settings}

Environment variables for this component of the Firezone server. For a
list of available variables, please refer to the [upstream definitions](https://github.com/firezone/firezone/blob/main/elixir/apps/domain/lib/domain/config/definitions.ex).
Some variables like `OUTBOUND_EMAIL_ADAPTER_OPTS` require json values
for which you can use `VAR = builtins.toJSON { /* ... */ }`.

This component will automatically inherit all variables defined via
[`services.firezone.server.settings`](#opt-services.firezone.server.settings) and
[`services.firezone.server.settingsSecret`](#opt-services.firezone.server.settingsSecret), but which can be
overwritten by this option.

*Type:* open submodule of attribute set of (boolean or floating point number or signed integer or string or absolute path or package)

*Default:* `{ }`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.enable` {#opt-services.firezone.server.enable}

Whether to enable all Firezone components.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.enableLocalDB` {#opt-services.firezone.server.enableLocalDB}

Whether to enable a local postgresql database for Firezone.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.nginx.enable` {#opt-services.firezone.server.nginx.enable}

Whether to enable nginx virtualhost definition.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.openClusterFirewall` {#opt-services.firezone.server.openClusterFirewall}

Opens up the erlang distribution port of all enabled components to
allow reaching the server cluster from the internet. You only need to
set this if you are actually distributing your cluster across multiple
machines.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.settings` {#opt-services.firezone.server.settings}

Environment variables for the Firezone server. For a list of available
variables, please refer to the [upstream definitions](https://github.com/firezone/firezone/blob/main/elixir/apps/domain/lib/domain/config/definitions.ex).
Some variables like `OUTBOUND_EMAIL_ADAPTER_OPTS` require json values
for which you can use `VAR = builtins.toJSON { /* ... */ }`.

Each component has an additional `settings` option which allows you to
override specific variables passed to that component.

*Type:* open submodule of attribute set of (boolean or floating point number or signed integer or string or absolute path or package)

*Default:* `{ }`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.settingsSecret` {#opt-services.firezone.server.settingsSecret}

This is a convenience option which allows you to set secret values for
environment variables by specifying a file which will contain the value
at runtime. Before starting the server, the content of each file will
be loaded into the respective environment variable.

Otherwise, this option is equivalent to
[`services.firezone.server.settings`](#opt-services.firezone.server.settings). Refer to the settings
option for more information regarding the actual variables and how
filtering rules are applied for each component.

*Type:* open submodule of attribute set of absolute path

*Default:* `{ }`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.settingsSecret.COOKIE_ENCRYPTION_SALT` {#opt-services.firezone.server.settingsSecret.COOKIE_ENCRYPTION_SALT}

A file containing a unique base64 encoded secret for the
`COOKIE_ENCRYPTION_SALT`. All Firezone components in your cluster must
use the same value.

If this is `null`, a shared value will automatically be generated
on startup and used for all components on this machine. You do
not need to set this except when you spread your cluster over
multiple hosts.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.settingsSecret.COOKIE_SIGNING_SALT` {#opt-services.firezone.server.settingsSecret.COOKIE_SIGNING_SALT}

A file containing a unique base64 encoded secret for the
`COOKIE_SIGNING_SALT`. All Firezone components in your cluster must
use the same value.

If this is `null`, a shared value will automatically be generated
on startup and used for all components on this machine. You do
not need to set this except when you spread your cluster over
multiple hosts.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.settingsSecret.LIVE_VIEW_SIGNING_SALT` {#opt-services.firezone.server.settingsSecret.LIVE_VIEW_SIGNING_SALT}

A file containing a unique base64 encoded secret for the
`LIVE_VIEW_SIGNING_SALT`. All Firezone components in your cluster must
use the same value.

If this is `null`, a shared value will automatically be generated
on startup and used for all components on this machine. You do
not need to set this except when you spread your cluster over
multiple hosts.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.settingsSecret.RELEASE_COOKIE` {#opt-services.firezone.server.settingsSecret.RELEASE_COOKIE}

A file containing a unique secret identifier for the Erlang
cluster. All Firezone components in your cluster must use the
same value.

If this is `null`, a shared value will automatically be generated
on startup and used for all components on this machine. You do
not need to set this except when you spread your cluster over
multiple hosts.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.settingsSecret.SECRET_KEY_BASE` {#opt-services.firezone.server.settingsSecret.SECRET_KEY_BASE}

A file containing a unique base64 encoded secret for the
`SECRET_KEY_BASE`. All Firezone components in your cluster must
use the same value.

If this is `null`, a shared value will automatically be generated
on startup and used for all components on this machine. You do
not need to set this except when you spread your cluster over
multiple hosts.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.settingsSecret.TOKENS_KEY_BASE` {#opt-services.firezone.server.settingsSecret.TOKENS_KEY_BASE}

A file containing a unique base64 encoded secret for the
`TOKENS_KEY_BASE`. All Firezone components in your cluster must
use the same value.

If this is `null`, a shared value will automatically be generated
on startup and used for all components on this machine. You do
not need to set this except when you spread your cluster over
multiple hosts.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.settingsSecret.TOKENS_SALT` {#opt-services.firezone.server.settingsSecret.TOKENS_SALT}

A file containing a unique base64 encoded secret for the
`TOKENS_SALT`. All Firezone components in your cluster must
use the same value.

If this is `null`, a shared value will automatically be generated
on startup and used for all components on this machine. You do
not need to set this except when you spread your cluster over
multiple hosts.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.smtp.configureManually` {#opt-services.firezone.server.smtp.configureManually}

Outbound email configuration is mandatory for Firezone and supports
many different delivery adapters. Yet, most users will only need an
SMTP relay to send emails, so this configuration enforced by default.

If you want to utilize an alternative way to send emails (e.g. via a
supportd API-based service), enable this option and define
`OUTBOUND_EMAIL_FROM`, `OUTBOUND_EMAIL_ADAPTER` and
`OUTBOUND_EMAIL_ADAPTER_OPTS` manually via
[`services.firezone.server.settings`](#opt-services.firezone.server.settings) and/or
[`services.firezone.server.settingsSecret`](#opt-services.firezone.server.settingsSecret).

The Firezone documentation holds [a list of supported Swoosh adapters](https://github.com/firezone/firezone/blob/main/website/src/app/docs/reference/env-vars/readme.mdx#outbound-emails).

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.smtp.from` {#opt-services.firezone.server.smtp.from}

Outbound SMTP FROM address

*Type:* string

*Example:* `"firezone@example.com"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.smtp.host` {#opt-services.firezone.server.smtp.host}

Outbound SMTP host

*Type:* string

*Example:* `"mail.example.com"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.smtp.implicitTls` {#opt-services.firezone.server.smtp.implicitTls}

Whether to use implicit TLS instead of STARTTLS (usually port 465)

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.smtp.passwordFile` {#opt-services.firezone.server.smtp.passwordFile}

File containing the password for the given username. Beware that a file in the nix store will be world readable.

*Type:* absolute path

*Example:* `"/run/secrets/smtp-password"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.smtp.port` {#opt-services.firezone.server.smtp.port}

Outbound SMTP port

*Type:* 16 bit unsigned integer; between 0 and 65535 (both inclusive)

*Example:* `465`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.smtp.username` {#opt-services.firezone.server.smtp.username}

Username to authenticate against the SMTP relay

*Type:* string

*Example:* `"firezone@example.com"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.web.address` {#opt-services.firezone.server.web.address}

The address to listen on

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"127.0.0.1"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.web.enable` {#opt-services.firezone.server.web.enable}

Whether to enable the Firezone web server.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.web.externalUrl` {#opt-services.firezone.server.web.externalUrl}

The external URL under which you will serve the web interface. You
need to setup a reverse proxy for TLS termination, either with
[`services.firezone.server.nginx.enable`](#opt-services.firezone.server.nginx.enable) or manually.

*Type:* string matching the pattern ^https://.+/$

*Example:* `"https://firezone.example.com/"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.web.package` {#opt-services.firezone.server.web.package}

The firezone-server-web package to use.

*Type:* package

*Default:* `pkgs.firezone-server-web`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.web.port` {#opt-services.firezone.server.web.port}

The port under which the web interface will be served locally

*Type:* 16 bit unsigned integer; between 0 and 65535 (both inclusive)

*Default:* `8080`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.web.settings` {#opt-services.firezone.server.web.settings}

Environment variables for this component of the Firezone server. For a
list of available variables, please refer to the [upstream definitions](https://github.com/firezone/firezone/blob/main/elixir/apps/domain/lib/domain/config/definitions.ex).
Some variables like `OUTBOUND_EMAIL_ADAPTER_OPTS` require json values
for which you can use `VAR = builtins.toJSON { /* ... */ }`.

This component will automatically inherit all variables defined via
[`services.firezone.server.settings`](#opt-services.firezone.server.settings) and
[`services.firezone.server.settingsSecret`](#opt-services.firezone.server.settingsSecret), but which can be
overwritten by this option.

*Type:* open submodule of attribute set of (boolean or floating point number or signed integer or string or absolute path or package)

*Default:* `{ }`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.firezone.server.web.trustedProxies` {#opt-services.firezone.server.web.trustedProxies}

A list of trusted proxies

*Type:* list of string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/firezone/server.nix)

## `services.flame.apps` {#opt-services.flame.apps}

Applications shown on the dashboard. When non-empty, this fully
replaces the contents of Flame's `apps` table on every service
start — any apps added through the web UI will not persist
across restarts.

*Type:* list of (submodule)

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:*

``` nix
[
  {
    name = "Router";
    url = "http://192.168.1.1";
  }
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix)

## `services.flame.apps.*.description` {#opt-services.flame.apps._.description}

Short description shown for the app.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `""`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix)

## `services.flame.apps.*.icon` {#opt-services.flame.apps._.icon}

Icon name or URL for the app.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"cancel"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix)

## `services.flame.apps.*.isPinned` {#opt-services.flame.apps._.isPinned}

Whether the app is pinned by default.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix)

## `services.flame.apps.*.name` {#opt-services.flame.apps._.name}

Name of the app.

*Type:* string

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix)

## `services.flame.apps.*.url` {#opt-services.flame.apps._.url}

URL of the app.

*Type:* string

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix)

## `services.flame.categories` {#opt-services.flame.categories}

Bookmark categories and their bookmarks. When non-empty, this
fully replaces the contents of Flame's `categories` and
`bookmarks` tables on every service start — any bookmarks added
through the web UI will not persist across restarts.

*Type:* list of (submodule)

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:*

``` nix
[
  {
    bookmarks = [
      {
        name = "GitHub";
        url = "https://github.com";
      }
    ];
    name = "Dev";
  }
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix)

## `services.flame.categories.*.bookmarks` {#opt-services.flame.categories._.bookmarks}

Bookmarks belonging to this category.

*Type:* list of (submodule)

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix)

## `services.flame.categories.*.bookmarks.*.icon` {#opt-services.flame.categories._.bookmarks._.icon}

Icon name or URL for the bookmark.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `""`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix)

## `services.flame.categories.*.bookmarks.*.name` {#opt-services.flame.categories._.bookmarks._.name}

Name of the bookmark.

*Type:* string

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix)

## `services.flame.categories.*.bookmarks.*.url` {#opt-services.flame.categories._.bookmarks._.url}

URL of the bookmark.

*Type:* string

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix)

## `services.flame.categories.*.isPinned` {#opt-services.flame.categories._.isPinned}

Whether the category is pinned by default.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix)

## `services.flame.categories.*.name` {#opt-services.flame.categories._.name}

Name of the bookmark category.

*Type:* string

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix)

## `services.flame.customCSS` {#opt-services.flame.customCSS}

Custom CSS injected into Flame's UI, written to
`public/flame.css` on every service start. Can also be used
to define a fully custom theme via CSS custom properties — see
[Flame's Custom CSS wiki page](https://github.com/pawelmalak/flame/wiki/Custom-CSS).

*Type:* strings concatenated with "\\n"

*Default:* `""`

*Example:*

``` nix
''
  .Home_SettingsButton__Qvn8C {
    border-radius: 0 !important;
  }
''
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix)

## `services.flame.enable` {#opt-services.flame.enable}

Whether to enable Flame, a self-hosted startpage for your server.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix)

## `services.flame.openFirewall` {#opt-services.flame.openFirewall}

Whether to open the firewall for the port used by Flame.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix)

## `services.flame.package` {#opt-services.flame.package}

The flame package to use.

*Type:* package

*Default:* `pkgs.flame`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix)

## `services.flame.passwordFile` {#opt-services.flame.passwordFile}

Path to a file containing the password to log in to Flame's settings panel.
This is the recommended option as it avoids storing the password in the Nix store.
Compatible with sops-nix and agenix.

*Type:* absolute path

*Example:* `"/run/secrets/flame-password"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix)

## `services.flame.port` {#opt-services.flame.port}

Port on which to serve the Flame web interface.

*Type:* 16 bit unsigned integer; between 0 and 65535 (both inclusive)

*Default:* `5005`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix)

## `services.flame.settings` {#opt-services.flame.settings}

Flame settings, written to Flame's settings JSON on every service
start. Accepts any key Flame's settings API supports; see
[Flame's source](https://github.com/pawelmalak/flame/blob/master/client/src/context/context.js)
for the current schema, since Flame does not publish separate
settings documentation.

`greetingsSchema`, `daySchema`, and `monthSchema` accept either a
list of strings or a single semicolon-separated string.

*Type:* open submodule of (JSON value)

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  customTitle = "My Dashboard";
  hideHeader = true;
  lat = 52.52;
  long = 13.405;
}
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix)

## `services.flame.settings.weatherApiKeyFile` {#opt-services.flame.settings.weatherApiKeyFile}

Path to a file containing the API key obtained from https://www.weatherapi.com used for
Flame's weather widget.
Compatible with sops-nix and agenix.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"/run/secrets/flame-weather-api-key"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flame.nix)

## `services.flannel.backend` {#opt-services.flannel.backend}

Type of backend to use and specific configurations for that backend.

*Type:* attribute set

*Default:*

``` nix
{
  Type = "vxlan";
}
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix)

## `services.flannel.enable` {#opt-services.flannel.enable}

Whether to enable flannel.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix)

## `services.flannel.etcd.caFile` {#opt-services.flannel.etcd.caFile}

Etcd certificate authority file

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix)

## `services.flannel.etcd.certFile` {#opt-services.flannel.etcd.certFile}

Etcd cert file

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix)

## `services.flannel.etcd.endpoints` {#opt-services.flannel.etcd.endpoints}

Etcd endpoints

*Type:* list of string

*Default:*

``` nix
[
  "http://127.0.0.1:2379"
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix)

## `services.flannel.etcd.keyFile` {#opt-services.flannel.etcd.keyFile}

Etcd key file

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix)

## `services.flannel.etcd.prefix` {#opt-services.flannel.etcd.prefix}

Etcd key prefix

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"/coreos.com/network"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix)

## `services.flannel.extraNetworkConfig` {#opt-services.flannel.extraNetworkConfig}

Extra configuration to be added to the net-conf.json/etcd-backed network configuration.

*Type:* JSON value

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  EnableIPv6 = true;
}
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix)

## `services.flannel.iface` {#opt-services.flannel.iface}

Interface to use (IP or name) for inter-host communication.
Defaults to the interface for the default route on the machine.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix)

## `services.flannel.kubeconfig` {#opt-services.flannel.kubeconfig}

Path to kubeconfig to use for storing flannel config using the
Kubernetes API

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix)

## `services.flannel.network` {#opt-services.flannel.network}

IPv4 network in CIDR format to use for the entire flannel network

*Type:* string

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix)

## `services.flannel.nodeName` {#opt-services.flannel.nodeName}

Needed when running with Kubernetes as backend as this cannot be auto-detected";

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `config.networking.fqdnOrHostName`

*Example:* `"node1.example.com"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix)

## `services.flannel.package` {#opt-services.flannel.package}

The flannel package to use.

*Type:* package

*Default:* `pkgs.flannel`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix)

## `services.flannel.publicIp` {#opt-services.flannel.publicIp}

IP accessible by other nodes for inter-host communication.
Defaults to the IP of the interface being used for communication.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix)

## `services.flannel.storageBackend` {#opt-services.flannel.storageBackend}

Determines where flannel stores its configuration at runtime

*Type:* one of "etcd", "kubernetes"

*Default:* `"etcd"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix)

## `services.flannel.subnetLen` {#opt-services.flannel.subnetLen}

The size of the subnet allocated to each host. Defaults to 24 (i.e. /24)
unless the Network was configured to be smaller than a /24 in which case
it is one less than the network.

*Type:* signed integer

*Default:* `24`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix)

## `services.flannel.subnetMax` {#opt-services.flannel.subnetMax}

The end of IP range which the subnet allocation should start with.
Defaults to the last subnet of Network.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix)

## `services.flannel.subnetMin` {#opt-services.flannel.subnetMin}

The beginning of IP range which the subnet allocation should start with.
Defaults to the first subnet of Network.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/networking/flannel.nix)

## `services.flap-alerted.enable` {#opt-services.flap-alerted.enable}

Whether to enable FlapAlerted.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/monitoring/flap-alerted.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/monitoring/flap-alerted.nix)

## `services.flap-alerted.environmentFiles` {#opt-services.flap-alerted.environmentFiles}

Files to load environment variables from.
This is useful to avoid putting secrets into the nix store.
See [https://github.com/Kioubit/FlapAlerted](https://github.com/Kioubit/FlapAlerted) for a list of options.

*Type:* list of absolute path

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:*

``` nix
[
  "/run/secrets/flap-alerted.env"
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/monitoring/flap-alerted.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/monitoring/flap-alerted.nix)

## `services.flap-alerted.extraArgs` {#opt-services.flap-alerted.extraArgs}

Extra command line arguments to pass to FlapAlerted.
See [https://github.com/Kioubit/FlapAlerted](https://github.com/Kioubit/FlapAlerted) for a list of options.

*Type:* list of string

*Default:* `[ ]`

The flap-alerted package to use.

*Default:* `pkgs.flap-alerted`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/monitoring/flap-alerted.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/monitoring/flap-alerted.nix)

## `services.flap-alerted.settings` {#opt-services.flap-alerted.settings}

Configuration of FlapAlerted.
See [https://github.com/Kioubit/FlapAlerted](https://github.com/Kioubit/FlapAlerted) for a list of options.

*Type:* open submodule of attribute set of (null or string or signed integer or boolean)

*Default:* `{ }`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/monitoring/flap-alerted.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/monitoring/flap-alerted.nix)

## `services.flap-alerted.settings.asn` {#opt-services.flap-alerted.settings.asn}

Your ASN number

*Type:* 32 bit unsigned integer; between 0 and 4294967295 (both inclusive)

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/monitoring/flap-alerted.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/monitoring/flap-alerted.nix)

## `services.flap-alerted.settings.bgpListenAddress` {#opt-services.flap-alerted.settings.bgpListenAddress}

Address to listen on for incoming BGP connections

*Type:* string

*Default:* `":1790"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/monitoring/flap-alerted.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/monitoring/flap-alerted.nix)

## `services.flap-alerted.settings.debug` {#opt-services.flap-alerted.settings.debug}

Open the port in the firewall for FlareSolverr.

The flaresolverr package to use.

The port on which FlareSolverr will listen for incoming HTTP traffic.
