## `services.flarum.adminEmail` {#opt-services.flarum.adminEmail}

Email for first web application administrator

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"admin@example.com"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix)

## `services.flarum.adminUser` {#opt-services.flarum.adminUser}

Username for first web application administrator

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"flarum"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix)

## `services.flarum.adoptConfig` {#opt-services.flarum.adoptConfig}

Whether to let this module manage a pre-existing config.php,
such as a hand-maintained one (typically with
`createDatabaseLocally` = false).

By default, a config.php this module didn't create is left alone:
`baseUrl` and `database` are not applied to it, so
real settings can't get silently overwritten by their defaults.

Before enabling this, make sure `baseUrl` and
`database` already match the file's real values. Once
enabled, config.php is regenerated from these options on every
activation, and anything in the file not covered by them is lost.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix)

## `services.flarum.baseUrl` {#opt-services.flarum.baseUrl}

Change `domain` instead.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"http://localhost"`

*Example:* `"https://forum.example.com"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix)

## `services.flarum.createDatabaseLocally` {#opt-services.flarum.createDatabaseLocally}

Create the database and database user locally, and run installation.

WARNING: Due to [https://github.com/flarum/framework/issues/4018](https://github.com/flarum/framework/issues/4018), this option is set
to false by default. The 'flarum install' command may delete existing database tables.
Only set this to true if you are certain you are working with a fresh, empty database.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix)

## `services.flarum.database` {#opt-services.flarum.database}

MySQL database parameters.

WARNING: A `password` set here is stored world-readable in the
Nix store. Use `databasePasswordFile` instead.

*Type:* open submodule of attribute set of (string or boolean or signed integer)

*Default:* `{ }`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix)

## `services.flarum.database.charset` {#opt-services.flarum.database.charset}

Character encoding for the database.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"utf8mb4"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix)

## `services.flarum.database.collation` {#opt-services.flarum.database.collation}

Character collation for database sorting and comparison.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"utf8mb4_unicode_ci"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix)

## `services.flarum.database.database` {#opt-services.flarum.database.database}

Database name.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"flarum"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix)

## `services.flarum.database.driver` {#opt-services.flarum.database.driver}

Database driver; i.e. MySQL, MariaDB...

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"mysql"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix)

## `services.flarum.database.engine` {#opt-services.flarum.database.engine}

Storage engine for new tables; MySQL-only.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"InnoDB"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix)

## `services.flarum.database.host` {#opt-services.flarum.database.host}

Database server hostname.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"localhost"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix)

## `services.flarum.database.password` {#opt-services.flarum.database.password}

Password for database server access.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `""`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix)

## `services.flarum.database.port` {#opt-services.flarum.database.port}

Database connection port; defaults to 3306 with MySQL.

*Type:* 16 bit unsigned integer; between 0 and 65535 (both inclusive)

*Default:* `3306`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix)

## `services.flarum.database.prefix` {#opt-services.flarum.database.prefix}

Table prefix; useful for sharing a database with other services.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `""`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix)

## `services.flarum.database.prefix_indexes` {#opt-services.flarum.database.prefix_indexes}

Apply table prefix to database index names.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix)

## `services.flarum.database.strict` {#opt-services.flarum.database.strict}

Enable strict SQL mode.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix)

## `services.flarum.database.username` {#opt-services.flarum.database.username}

Username for database server access.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"flarum"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix)

## `services.flarum.databasePasswordFile` {#opt-services.flarum.databasePasswordFile}

File containing the database password.
Must be readable by the flarum user.
Takes precedence over `database.password`.

The password must not contain `"` or `\` characters, as it is
substituted into a JSON installation config verbatim.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"/run/secrets/flarum-db-password"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix)

## `services.flarum.domain` {#opt-services.flarum.domain}

Domain to serve on.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"localhost"`

*Example:* `"forum.example.com"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix)

## `services.flarum.enable` {#opt-services.flarum.enable}

Whether to enable Flarum discussion platform.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix)

## `services.flarum.forumTitle` {#opt-services.flarum.forumTitle}

Title of the forum.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"A Flarum Forum on NixOS"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix)

## `services.flarum.group` {#opt-services.flarum.group}

System group to run Flarum

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"flarum"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix)

## `services.flarum.initialAdminPassword` {#opt-services.flarum.initialAdminPassword}

Initial password for the adminUser.

WARNING: This is stored world-readable in the Nix store.
Use `initialAdminPasswordFile` instead.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"flarum"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix)

## `services.flarum.initialAdminPasswordFile` {#opt-services.flarum.initialAdminPasswordFile}

File containing the initial password for adminUser.
Must be readable by the flarum user.
Takes precedence over `initialAdminPassword`.

The password must not contain `"` or `\` characters, as it is
substituted into a JSON installation config verbatim.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"/run/secrets/flarum-admin-password"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix)

## `services.flarum.package` {#opt-services.flarum.package}

Flarum package to use.

Upgrading from Flarum 1.x to 2.x migrates the database and can't be
undone, so back up the database and make sure all extensions support
Flarum 2 before switching.

*Type:* package

*Default:*

``` nix
if versionOlder config.system.stateVersion "26.11" then pkgs.flarum_1 else pkgs.flarum_2
```

*Related packages:*

- [`pkgs.flarum_1`](https://search.nixos.org/packages?show=flarum_1&sort=relevance&query=flarum_1)
- [`pkgs.flarum_2`](https://search.nixos.org/packages?show=flarum_2&sort=relevance&query=flarum_2)

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix)

## `services.flarum.stateDir` {#opt-services.flarum.stateDir}

Home directory for writable storage

*Type:* absolute path

*Default:* `"/var/lib/flarum"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix)

## `services.flarum.user` {#opt-services.flarum.user}

System user to run Flarum

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"flarum"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/flarum.nix)

## `services.flatpak.enable` {#opt-services.flatpak.enable}

Whether to enable flatpak.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/desktops/flatpak.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/desktops/flatpak.nix)

## `services.flatpak.package` {#opt-services.flatpak.package}

The flatpak package to use.

*Type:* package

*Default:* `pkgs.flatpak`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/desktops/flatpak.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/desktops/flatpak.nix)

## `services.flexget.config` {#opt-services.flexget.config}

The YAML configuration for FlexGet.

*Type:* strings concatenated with "\\n"

*Default:* `""`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/torrent/flexget.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/torrent/flexget.nix)

## `services.flexget.enable` {#opt-services.flexget.enable}

Whether to enable FlexGet daemon.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/torrent/flexget.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/torrent/flexget.nix)

## `services.flexget.homeDir` {#opt-services.flexget.homeDir}

Where files live.

*Type:* absolute path

*Default:* `"/var/lib/deluge"`

*Example:* `"/home/flexget"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/torrent/flexget.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/torrent/flexget.nix)

## `services.flexget.interval` {#opt-services.flexget.interval}

When to perform a `flexget` run. See `man 7 systemd.time` for the format.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"10m"`

*Example:* `"1h"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/torrent/flexget.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/torrent/flexget.nix)

## `services.flexget.package` {#opt-services.flexget.package}

The flexget package to use.

*Type:* package

*Default:* `pkgs.flexget`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/torrent/flexget.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/torrent/flexget.nix)

## `services.flexget.systemScheduler` {#opt-services.flexget.systemScheduler}

When true, execute the runs via the flexget-runner.timer. If false, you have to specify the settings yourself in the YML file.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `true`

*Example:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/torrent/flexget.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/torrent/flexget.nix)

## `services.flexget.user` {#opt-services.flexget.user}

The user under which to run flexget.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"deluge"`

*Example:* `"some_user"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/torrent/flexget.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/torrent/flexget.nix)

## `services.flood.enable` {#opt-services.flood.enable}

Whether to enable flood.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/torrent/flood.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/torrent/flood.nix)

## `services.flood.extraArgs` {#opt-services.flood.extraArgs}

Extra arguments passed to `flood`.

*Type:* list of string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:*

``` nix
[
  "--baseuri=/"
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/torrent/flood.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/torrent/flood.nix)

## `services.flood.host` {#opt-services.flood.host}

Host to bind webserver.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"localhost"`

*Example:* `"::"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/torrent/flood.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/torrent/flood.nix)

## `services.flood.openFirewall` {#opt-services.flood.openFirewall}

Whether to open the firewall for the port in [`services.flood.port`](#opt-services.flood.port).

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/torrent/flood.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/torrent/flood.nix)

## `services.flood.package` {#opt-services.flood.package}

The flood package to use.

*Type:* package

*Default:* `pkgs.flood`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/torrent/flood.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/torrent/flood.nix)

## `services.flood.port` {#opt-services.flood.port}

Port to bind webserver.

*Type:* 16 bit unsigned integer; between 0 and 65535 (both inclusive)

*Default:* `3000`

*Example:* `3001`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/torrent/flood.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/torrent/flood.nix)

## `services.fluent-bit.configurationFile` {#opt-services.fluent-bit.configurationFile}

Fluent Bit configuration. See
[https://docs.fluentbit.io/manual/administration/configuring-fluent-bit/yaml](https://docs.fluentbit.io/manual/administration/configuring-fluent-bit/yaml)
for supported values.

`configurationFile` takes precedence over `settings`.

Note: Restricted evaluation blocks access to paths outside the Nix store.
This means detecting content changes for mutable paths (i.e. not input or content-addressed) can't be done.
As a result, `nixos-rebuild` won't reload/restart the systemd unit when mutable path contents change.
`systemctl restart fluent-bit.service` must be used instead.

*Type:* absolute path

*Default:* `yamlFormat.generate "fluent-bit.yaml" cfg.settings`

*Example:* `"/etc/fluent-bit/fluent-bit.yaml"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/monitoring/fluent-bit.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/monitoring/fluent-bit.nix)

## `services.fluent-bit.enable` {#opt-services.fluent-bit.enable}

Whether to enable Fluent Bit.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/monitoring/fluent-bit.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/monitoring/fluent-bit.nix)

## `services.fluent-bit.graceLimit` {#opt-services.fluent-bit.graceLimit}

The grace time limit. Sets the systemd unit's `TimeoutStopSec`.

The `service.grace` option in the Fluent Bit configuration should be ≤ this option.

*Type:* null or positive integer, meaning \>0, or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `30`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/monitoring/fluent-bit.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/monitoring/fluent-bit.nix)

## `services.fluent-bit.package` {#opt-services.fluent-bit.package}

The fluent-bit package to use.

*Type:* package

*Default:* `pkgs.fluent-bit`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/monitoring/fluent-bit.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/monitoring/fluent-bit.nix)

## `services.fluent-bit.settings` {#opt-services.fluent-bit.settings}

See `configurationFile`.

`configurationFile` takes precedence over `settings`.

*Type:* YAML 1.1 value

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  pipeline = {
    inputs = [
      {
        name = "systemd";
        systemd_filter = "_SYSTEMD_UNIT=fluent-bit.service";
      }
    ];
    outputs = [
      {
        file = "fluent-bit.out";
        name = "file";
        path = "/var/log/fluent-bit";
      }
    ];
  };
  service = {
    grace = 30;
  };
}
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/monitoring/fluent-bit.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/monitoring/fluent-bit.nix)

## `services.fluentd.config` {#opt-services.fluentd.config}

Fluentd config.

*Type:* strings concatenated with "\\n"

*Default:* `""`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/logging/fluentd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/logging/fluentd.nix)

## `services.fluentd.enable` {#opt-services.fluentd.enable}

Whether to enable fluentd, a data/log collector.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/logging/fluentd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/logging/fluentd.nix)

## `services.fluentd.package` {#opt-services.fluentd.package}

The fluentd package to use.

*Type:* package

*Default:* `pkgs.fluentd`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/logging/fluentd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/logging/fluentd.nix)

## `services.fluentd.plugins` {#opt-services.fluentd.plugins}

A list of plugin paths to pass into fluentd. It will make plugins defined in ruby files
there available in your config.

*Type:* list of absolute path

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/logging/fluentd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/logging/fluentd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.enable` {#opt-services.fluidd.enable}

Whether to enable Fluidd, a Klipper web interface for managing your 3d printer.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.hostName` {#opt-services.fluidd.hostName}

Hostname to serve fluidd on

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"localhost"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx}

Extra configuration for the nginx virtual host of fluidd.

*Type:* submodule

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  serverAliases = [ "fluidd.${config.networking.domain}" ];
}
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.acmeFallbackHost` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.acmeFallbackHost}

Host which to proxy requests to if ACME challenge is not found. Useful
if you want multiple hosts to be able to verify the same domain name.

With this option, you could request certificates for the present domain
with an ACME client that is running on another host, which you would
specify here.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.acmeRoot` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.acmeRoot}

Directory for the ACME challenge, which is **public**. Don't put certs or keys in here.
Set to null to inherit from config.security.acme.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `"/var/lib/acme/acme-challenge"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.addSSL` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.addSSL}

Whether to enable HTTPS in addition to plain HTTP. This will set defaults for
`listen` to listen on all interfaces on the respective default
ports (80, 443).

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.basicAuth` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.basicAuth}

Basic Auth protection for a vhost.

WARNING: This is implemented to store the password in plain text in the
Nix store.

*Type:* attribute set of string

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  user = "password";
};
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.basicAuthFile` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.basicAuthFile}

Basic Auth password file for a vhost.
Can be created by running `nix-shell --packages apacheHttpd --run 'htpasswd -B -c FILENAME USERNAME'`.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.default` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.default}

Makes this vhost the default.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.enableACME` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.enableACME}

Whether to ask Let's Encrypt to sign a certificate for this vhost.
Alternately, you can use an existing certificate through `useACMEHost`.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.extraConfig` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.extraConfig}

These lines go to the end of the vhost verbatim.

*Type:* strings concatenated with "\\n"

*Default:* `""`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.forceSSL` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.forceSSL}

Whether to add a separate nginx server block that redirects (defaults
to 301, configurable with `redirectCode`) all plain HTTP traffic to
HTTPS. This will set defaults for `listen` to listen on all interfaces
on the respective default ports (80, 443), where the non-SSL listens
are used for the redirect vhosts.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.globalRedirect` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.globalRedirect}

If set, all requests for this host are redirected (defaults to 301,
configurable with `redirectCode`) to the given hostname.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"newserver.example.org"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.http2` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.http2}

Whether to enable the HTTP/2 protocol.
Note that (as of writing) due to nginx's implementation, to disable
HTTP/2 you have to disable it on all vhosts that use a given
IP address / port.
If there is one server block configured to enable http2, then it is
enabled for all server blocks on this IP.
See [https://stackoverflow.com/a/39466948/263061](https://stackoverflow.com/a/39466948/263061).

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.http3` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.http3}

Whether to enable the HTTP/3 protocol.
This requires activating the QUIC transport protocol
`services.nginx.virtualHosts.<name>.quic = true;`.
Note that HTTP/3 support is experimental and *not* yet recommended for production.
Read more at [https://quic.nginx.org/](https://quic.nginx.org/)
HTTP/3 availability must be manually advertised, preferably in each location block.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.http3_hq` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.http3_hq}

Whether to enable the HTTP/0.9 protocol negotiation used in QUIC interoperability tests.
This requires activating the QUIC transport protocol
`services.nginx.virtualHosts.<name>.quic = true;`.
Note that special application protocol support is experimental and *not* yet recommended for production.
Read more at [https://quic.nginx.org/](https://quic.nginx.org/)

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.kTLS` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.kTLS}

Whether to enable kTLS support.
Implementing TLS in the kernel (kTLS) improves performance by significantly
reducing the need for copying operations between user space and the kernel.
Required Nginx version 1.21.4 or later.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.listen` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.listen}

Listen addresses and ports for this virtual host.
IPv6 addresses must be enclosed in square brackets.
Note: this option overrides `addSSL`
and `onlySSL`.

If you only want to set the addresses manually and not
the ports, take a look at `listenAddresses`.

*Type:* list of (submodule)

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:*

``` nix
[
  {
    addr = "195.154.1.1";
    port = 443;
    ssl = true;
  }
  {
    addr = "192.154.1.1";
    port = 80;
  }
  {
    addr = "unix:/var/run/nginx.sock";
  }
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.listen.*.addr` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.listen._.addr}

Listen address.

*Type:* string

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.listen.*.extraParameters` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.listen._.extraParameters}

Extra parameters of this listen directive.

*Type:* list of string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:*

``` nix
[
  "backlog=1024"
  "deferred"
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.listen.*.port` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.listen._.port}

Port number to listen on.
If unset and the listen address is not a socket then nginx defaults to 80.

*Type:* null or 16 bit unsigned integer; between 0 and 65535 (both inclusive)

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.listen.*.proxyProtocol` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.listen._.proxyProtocol}

Enable PROXY protocol.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.listen.*.ssl` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.listen._.ssl}

Enable SSL.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.listenAddresses` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.listenAddresses}

Listen addresses for this virtual host.
Compared to `listen` this only sets the addresses
and the ports are chosen automatically.

Note: This option overrides `networking.enableIPv6`

*Type:* list of string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:*

``` nix
[
  "127.0.0.1"
  "[::1]"
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.locations` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.locations}

Declarative location config

*Type:* attribute set of (submodule)

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  "/" = {
    proxyPass = "http://localhost:3000";
  };
};
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.locations.<name>.alias` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.locations._name_.alias}

Alias directory for requests.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"/your/alias/directory"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.locations.<name>.basicAuth` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.locations._name_.basicAuth}

Basic Auth protection for a vhost.

WARNING: This is implemented to store the password in plain text in the
Nix store.

*Type:* attribute set of string

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  user = "password";
};
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.locations.<name>.basicAuthFile` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.locations._name_.basicAuthFile}

Basic Auth password file for a vhost.
Can be created by running `nix-shell --packages apacheHttpd --run 'htpasswd -B -c FILENAME USERNAME'`.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.locations.<name>.extraConfig` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.locations._name_.extraConfig}

These lines go to the end of the location verbatim.

*Type:* strings concatenated with "\\n"

*Default:* `""`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.locations.<name>.fastcgiParams` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.locations._name_.fastcgiParams}

FastCGI parameters to override.  Unlike in the Nginx
configuration file, overriding only some default parameters
won't unset the default values for other parameters.

*Type:* attribute set of (string or absolute path)

*Default:* `{ }`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.locations.<name>.index` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.locations._name_.index}

Adds index directive.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"index.php index.html"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.locations.<name>.priority` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.locations._name_.priority}

Order of this location block in relation to the others in the vhost.
The semantics are the same as with `lib.mkOrder`. Smaller values have
a greater priority.

*Type:* signed integer

*Default:* `1000`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.locations.<name>.proxyPass` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.locations._name_.proxyPass}

Adds proxy\_pass directive and sets recommended proxy headers if
recommendedProxySettings is enabled.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"http://www.example.org/"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.locations.<name>.proxyWebsockets` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.locations._name_.proxyWebsockets}

Whether to support proxying websocket connections with HTTP/1.1.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.locations.<name>.recommendedProxySettings` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.locations._name_.recommendedProxySettings}

Enable recommended proxy settings.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `config.services.nginx.recommendedProxySettings`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.locations.<name>.recommendedUwsgiSettings` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.locations._name_.recommendedUwsgiSettings}

Enable recommended uwsgi settings.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `config.services.nginx.recommendedUwsgiSettings`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.locations.<name>.return` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.locations._name_.return}

Adds a return directive, for e.g. redirections.

*Type:* null or string or signed integer

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"301 http://example.com$request_uri"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.locations.<name>.root` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.locations._name_.root}

Root directory for requests.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"/your/root/directory"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.locations.<name>.tryFiles` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.locations._name_.tryFiles}

Adds try\_files directive.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"$uri =404"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.locations.<name>.useGrpcErrorPages` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.locations._name_.useGrpcErrorPages}

Whether to configure error codes to be emitted as gRPC-compatible errors.

Should be set when proxying gRPC, and returning responses from nginx (like when adding authentication).

This defines a few `@grpc-*` locations inside the containing vhost.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.locations.<name>.uwsgiPass` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.locations._name_.uwsgiPass}

Adds uwsgi\_pass directive and sets recommended proxy headers if
recommendedUwsgiSettings is enabled.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"unix:/run/example/example.sock"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.onlySSL` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.onlySSL}

Whether to enable HTTPS and reject plain HTTP connections. This will set
defaults for `listen` to listen on all interfaces on port 443.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.quic` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.quic}

Whether to enable the QUIC transport protocol.
Note that QUIC support is experimental and
*not* yet recommended for production.
Read more at [https://quic.nginx.org/](https://quic.nginx.org/)

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.redirectCode` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.redirectCode}

HTTP status used by `globalRedirect` and `forceSSL`. Possible usecases
include temporary (302, 307) redirects, keeping the request method and
body (307, 308), or explicitly resetting the method to GET (303).
See [https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTTP/Redirections](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTTP/Redirections).

*Type:* integer between 300 and 399 (both inclusive)

*Default:* `301`

*Example:* `308`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.rejectSSL` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.rejectSSL}

Whether to listen for and reject all HTTPS connections to this vhost. Useful in
[default](../../options/services-n/index.md#opt-services.nginx.virtualHosts._name_.default)
server blocks to avoid serving the certificate for another vhost. Uses the
`ssl_reject_handshake` directive available in nginx versions
1.19.4 and above.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.reuseport` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.reuseport}

Create an individual listening socket .
It is required to specify only once on one of the hosts.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.root` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.root}

The path of the web root directory.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"/data/webserver/docs"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.serverAliases` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.serverAliases}

Additional names of virtual hosts served by this virtual host configuration.

*Type:* list of string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:*

``` nix
[
  "www.example.org"
  "example.org"
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.serverName` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.serverName}

Name of this virtual host. Defaults to attribute name in virtualHosts.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"example.org"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.sslCertificate` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.sslCertificate}

Path to server SSL certificate.

*Type:* absolute path

*Example:* `"/var/host.cert"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.sslCertificateKey` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.sslCertificateKey}

Path to server SSL certificate key.

*Type:* absolute path

*Example:* `"/var/host.key"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.sslTrustedCertificate` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.sslTrustedCertificate}

Path to root SSL certificate for stapling and client certificates.

*Type:* null or absolute path

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"${pkgs.cacert}/etc/ssl/certs/ca-bundle.crt"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.nginx.useACMEHost` {#opt-services.fluidd.nginx.useACMEHost}

A host of an existing Let's Encrypt certificate to use.
This is useful if you have many subdomains and want to avoid hitting the
[rate limit](https://letsencrypt.org/docs/rate-limits).
Alternately, you can generate a certificate through `enableACME`.
*Note that this option does not create any certificates, nor it does add subdomains to existing ones – you will need to create them manually using [security](../../options/security/index.md#opt-security.acme.certs).*

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.fluidd.package` {#opt-services.fluidd.package}

The fluidd package to use.

*Type:* package

*Default:* `pkgs.fluidd`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/web-apps/fluidd.nix)

## `services.foldingathome.daemonNiceLevel` {#opt-services.foldingathome.daemonNiceLevel}

Daemon process priority for FAHClient.
0 is the default Unix process priority, 19 is the lowest.

*Type:* integer between -20 and 19 (both inclusive)

*Default:* `0`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/computing/foldingathome/client.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/computing/foldingathome/client.nix)

## `services.foldingathome.enable` {#opt-services.foldingathome.enable}

Whether to enable Folding@home client.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/computing/foldingathome/client.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/computing/foldingathome/client.nix)

## `services.foldingathome.extraArgs` {#opt-services.foldingathome.extraArgs}

Extra startup options for the FAHClient. Run
`fah-client --help` to find all the available options.

*Type:* list of string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/computing/foldingathome/client.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/computing/foldingathome/client.nix)

## `services.foldingathome.package` {#opt-services.foldingathome.package}

The fahclient package to use.

*Type:* package

*Default:* `pkgs.fahclient`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/computing/foldingathome/client.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/computing/foldingathome/client.nix)

## `services.foldingathome.team` {#opt-services.foldingathome.team}

The team ID associated with the reported computation results. This
will be used in the ranking statistics.

By default, use the NixOS folding@home team ID is being used.

*Type:* signed integer

*Default:* `236565`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/computing/foldingathome/client.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/computing/foldingathome/client.nix)

## `services.foldingathome.user` {#opt-services.foldingathome.user}

The user associated with the reported computation results. This will
be used in the ranking statistics.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/computing/foldingathome/client.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/computing/foldingathome/client.nix)

## `services.forgejo-runner.instances` {#opt-services.forgejo-runner.instances}

Forgejo Runner instances.

*Type:* attribute set of (submodule)

*Default:* `{ }`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/continuous-integration/forgejo-runner.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/continuous-integration/forgejo-runner.nix)

## `services.forgejo-runner.instances.<name>.enable` {#opt-services.forgejo-runner.instances._name_.enable}

Whether to enable this Forgejo Runner instance.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/continuous-integration/forgejo-runner.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/continuous-integration/forgejo-runner.nix)

## `services.forgejo-runner.instances.<name>.hostPackages` {#opt-services.forgejo-runner.instances._name_.hostPackages}

List of packages that are available to your workflow and actions when the
runner is configured with a label of type `host` ([`services.forgejo-runner.instances.<name>.runtimes.host`](#opt-services.forgejo-runner.instances._name_.runtimes.host)).

::: note
`gitMinimal` is always part of the environment because `forgejo-runner`
depends on it. If you need a different variant of `git`, e.g. `gitFull`,
add it here. Your package will take priority over `gitMinimal`.
:::

*Type:* list of package

*Default:*

``` nix
with pkgs; [
  bash
  coreutils
  curl
  gawk
  gnused
  nodejs
  wget
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/continuous-integration/forgejo-runner.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/continuous-integration/forgejo-runner.nix)

## `services.forgejo-runner.instances.<name>.runtimes.docker` {#opt-services.forgejo-runner.instances._name_.runtimes.docker}

Whether to configure the systemd service to work with Docker.

::: warning
Setting this will override the automatic detection and safeguards.
:::

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* Whether this instance has at least one label with infix `:docker`
and `config.virtualisation.docker.enable` set to `true`.

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/continuous-integration/forgejo-runner.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/continuous-integration/forgejo-runner.nix)

## `services.forgejo-runner.instances.<name>.runtimes.host` {#opt-services.forgejo-runner.instances._name_.runtimes.host}

Whether to configure the systemd service for jobs with the backend of type `host`.

::: warning
Setting this will override the automatic detection and safeguards.
:::

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* Whether this instance has at least one label with suffix `:host`.

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/continuous-integration/forgejo-runner.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/continuous-integration/forgejo-runner.nix)

## `services.forgejo-runner.instances.<name>.runtimes.podman` {#opt-services.forgejo-runner.instances._name_.runtimes.podman}

Whether to configure the systemd service to work with Podman.

::: warning
Setting this will override the automatic detection and safeguards.
:::

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* Whether this instance has at least one label with infix `:docker`
and `config.virtualisation.podman.enable` set to `true`.

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/continuous-integration/forgejo-runner.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/continuous-integration/forgejo-runner.nix)

## `services.forgejo-runner.instances.<name>.secrets` {#opt-services.forgejo-runner.instances._name_.secrets}

This follows the same structure as [`services.forgejo-runner.instances.<name>.settings`](#opt-services.forgejo-runner.instances._name_.settings)
but the value of each key is a path.

The specified secret path is then read by systemd via [`LoadCredential=`](https://www.freedesktop.org/software/systemd/man/latest/systemd.exec.html#Credentials)
and templated into [`services.forgejo-runner.instances.<name>.settings`](#opt-services.forgejo-runner.instances._name_.settings) for you.

*Type:* nested attribute set of absolute path not in the Nix store

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  server.connections.example = {
    token_url = "/run/keys/forgejo-runner_token";
  };

  cache = {
    secret_url = "/run/keys/forgejo-runner_cache-secret";
  };
}
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/continuous-integration/forgejo-runner.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/continuous-integration/forgejo-runner.nix)

## `services.forgejo-runner.instances.<name>.settings` {#opt-services.forgejo-runner.instances._name_.settings}

Free-form settings written directly to the `config.yaml` file.
Refer to [`config.example.yaml`](https://code.forgejo.org/forgejo/runner/src/branch/main/internal/pkg/config/config.example.yaml) or run `forgejo-runner generation-config` for supported values.

*Type:* open submodule of (YAML 1.1 value)

*Default:* `{ }`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/continuous-integration/forgejo-runner.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/continuous-integration/forgejo-runner.nix)

## `services.forgejo-runner.instances.<name>.settings.runner.labels` {#opt-services.forgejo-runner.instances._name_.settings.runner.labels}

Labels used to map jobs to their runtime environment.

Many common actions require `bash`, `git` and `node`,
as well as a filesystem that follows the filesystem hierarchy standard.

If you specify a label of type `docker`, the resulting runner service
will be automatically added to the *Podman* or *Docker* group.

See [https://forgejo.org/docs/latest/admin/actions/configuration/#choosing-labels](https://forgejo.org/docs/latest/admin/actions/configuration/#choosing-labels).

::: note
Labels of type [`lxc`](https://forgejo.org/docs/latest/admin/actions/configuration/#lxc) are currently not supported.
:::

*Type:* list of string

*Default:* `[ ]`

*Example:*

``` nix
[
  # provide a debian base with nodejs for actions
  "debian-latest:docker://node:current"
  # fake the ubuntu name, because node provides no ubuntu builds
  "ubuntu-latest:docker://node:current"
  # provide native execution on the host
  #"native:host"
]
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/continuous-integration/forgejo-runner.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/continuous-integration/forgejo-runner.nix)

## `services.forgejo-runner.instances.<name>.settings.server.connections` {#opt-services.forgejo-runner.instances._name_.settings.server.connections}

One or more connections to Forgejo instances, each with a UUID and Token pair.

See [https://forgejo.org/docs/latest/admin/actions/registration/](https://forgejo.org/docs/latest/admin/actions/registration/).

::: note
Ephemeral runner mode is not yet supported by this module.
:::

*Type:* attribute set of (open submodule of (YAML 1.1 value))

*Default:* `{ }`

*Example:*

``` nix
{
  default = {
    url = "https://example.com/";
    uuid = "c9e50be9-a7c3-4aee-ba35-624c4ff8c519";

    # Also see services.forgejo-runner.instances.<name>.secrets.server.connections.<name>.token_url
    token = "6634bb58be0db23cc013a2e72dd1828ae0257cf";
  };
}
```

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/continuous-integration/forgejo-runner.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/continuous-integration/forgejo-runner.nix)

## `services.forgejo-runner.instances.<name>.settings.server.connections.<name>.token` {#opt-services.forgejo-runner.instances._name_.settings.server.connections._name_.token}

Token of this runner.

See [https://forgejo.org/docs/latest/admin/actions/registration/](https://forgejo.org/docs/latest/admin/actions/registration/).

::: note
The deprecated "Registration Token" is not supported.
You need a UUID and Token pair.
:::

::: warning
The value will be stored unencrypted in the world-readable Nix store.
To store the secret securely, see `services.forgejo-runner.instances.<name>.secrets.server.connections.<name>.token_url`.
:::

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"6634bb58be0db23cc013a2e72dd1828ae0257cf"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/continuous-integration/forgejo-runner.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/continuous-integration/forgejo-runner.nix)

## `services.forgejo-runner.instances.<name>.settings.server.connections.<name>.url` {#opt-services.forgejo-runner.instances._name_.settings.server.connections._name_.url}

Base URL of your Forgejo instance.

*Type:* string

*Example:* `"https://example.com/"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/continuous-integration/forgejo-runner.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/continuous-integration/forgejo-runner.nix)

## `services.forgejo-runner.instances.<name>.settings.server.connections.<name>.uuid` {#opt-services.forgejo-runner.instances._name_.settings.server.connections._name_.uuid}

UUID of this runner.

See [https://forgejo.org/docs/latest/admin/actions/registration/](https://forgejo.org/docs/latest/admin/actions/registration/).

*Type:* string

*Example:* `"c9e50be9-a7c3-4aee-ba35-624c4ff8c519"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/continuous-integration/forgejo-runner.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/continuous-integration/forgejo-runner.nix)

The forgejo-runner package to use.

Base directory for custom templates and other options.

Database host address.

## `services.forgejo.database.name` {#opt-services.forgejo.database.name}

Database name.

A file containing the password corresponding to

Database host port.

Path to the unix socket file to use for authentication.

Database engine to use.

*Default:* `"sqlite3"`

*Example:* `"mysql"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/misc/forgejo.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/misc/forgejo.nix)

## `services.forgejo.database.user` {#opt-services.forgejo.database.user}

Database user.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"forgejo"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/misc/forgejo.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/misc/forgejo.nix)

## `services.forgejo.dump.age` {#opt-services.forgejo.dump.age}

Age of backup used to decide what files to delete when cleaning.
If a file or directory is older than the current time minus the age field, it is deleted.

The format is described in
`tmpfiles.d(5)`.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"4w"`

*Example:* `"5d"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/misc/forgejo.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/misc/forgejo.nix)

## `services.forgejo.dump.backupDir` {#opt-services.forgejo.dump.backupDir}

Path to the directory where the dump archives will be stored.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"${config.services.forgejo.stateDir}/dump"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/misc/forgejo.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/misc/forgejo.nix)

## `services.forgejo.dump.enable` {#opt-services.forgejo.dump.enable}

Whether to enable periodic dumps via the [built-in `dump` command](https://forgejo.org/docs/latest/admin/command-line/#dump).

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

## `services.forgejo.dump.file` {#opt-services.forgejo.dump.file}

Filename to be used for the dump. If `null` a default name is chosen by forgejo.

*Type:* null or string

*Default:* `null`

*Example:* `"forgejo-dump"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/misc/forgejo.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/misc/forgejo.nix)

## `services.forgejo.dump.interval` {#opt-services.forgejo.dump.interval}

Run a Forgejo dump at this interval. Runs by default at 04:31 every day.

The format is described in
`systemd.time(7)`.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"04:31"`

*Example:* `"hourly"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/misc/forgejo.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/misc/forgejo.nix)

## `services.forgejo.dump.type` {#opt-services.forgejo.dump.type}

Archive format used to store the dump file.

*Type:* one of "zip", "tar", "tar.sz", "tar.gz", "tar.xz", "tar.bz2", "tar.br", "tar.lz4", "tar.zst"

*Default:* `"zip"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/misc/forgejo.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/misc/forgejo.nix)

## `services.forgejo.enable` {#opt-services.forgejo.enable}

Whether to enable Forgejo, a software forge.

*Type:* boolean

*Default:* `false`

*Example:* `true`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/misc/forgejo.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/misc/forgejo.nix)

## `services.forgejo.group` {#opt-services.forgejo.group}

Group under which Forgejo runs.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"forgejo"`

*Declared by:* [`nixos/modules/services/misc/forgejo.nix`](https://github.com/NixOS/nixpkgs/blob/master/nixos/modules/services/misc/forgejo.nix)

Where to store LFS files.

*Default:* `"${config.services.forgejo.stateDir}/data/lfs"`

## `services.forgejo.lfs.enable` {#opt-services.forgejo.lfs.enable}

Enables git-lfs support.

*Type:* boolean

The forgejo-lts package to use.

Path to the git repositories.

*Type:* string

*Default:* `"${config.services.forgejo.stateDir}/repositories"`
